/opt/homebrew/bin/zsh -lc 'cat /Users/nicholas/.agents/skills/code-review/SKILL.md' in /Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i06-free-energy-matched
 succeeded in 0ms:
---
name: code-review
description: Review the changes since a fixed point (commit, branch, tag, or merge-base) along two axes — Standards (does the code follow this repo's documented coding standards?) and Spec (does the code match what the originating issue/PRD asked for?). Runs both reviews in parallel sub-agents and reports them side by side. Use when the user wants to review a branch, a PR, work-in-progress changes, or asks to "review since X".
---

Two-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:

- **Standards** — does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?
- **Spec** — does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / PRD / spec?

Both axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.

The issue tracker should have been provided to you — run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` if `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing.

## Process

### 1. Pin the fixed point

Whatever the user said is the fixed point — a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc. If they didn't specify one, ask for it.

Capture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.

Before going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here — not inside two parallel sub-agents.

### 2. Identify the spec source

Look for the originating spec, in this order:

1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.) — fetch via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.
2. A path the user passed as an argument.
3. A PRD/spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.
4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report "no spec available".

### 3. Identify the standards sources

Anything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.

On top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below — a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:

- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.
- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic ("possible Feature Envy"), never a hard violation — and, like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.

Each smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:

- **Mysterious Name** — a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.
- **Duplicated Code** — the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.
- **Feature Envy** — a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.
- **Data Clumps** — the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.
- **Primitive Obsession** — a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.
- **Repeated Switches** — the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.
- **Shotgun Surgery** — one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.
- **Divergent Change** — one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.
- **Speculative Generality** — abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.
- **Message Chains** — long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.
- **Middle Man** — a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.
- **Refused Bequest** — a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.

### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel

Send a single message with two `Agent` tool calls. Use the `general-purpose` subagent for both.

**Standards sub-agent prompt** — include:

- The full diff command and commit list.
- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full — the sub-agent has no other access to it.
- The brief: "Report — per file/hunk where relevant — (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls — documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words."

**Spec sub-agent prompt** — include:

- The diff command and commit list.
- The path or fetched contents of the spec.
- The brief: "Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words."

If the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.

### 5. Aggregate

Present the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings — the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).

End with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes — that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.

## Why two axes

A change can pass one axis and fail the other:

- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**
- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**

Reporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.

## Freeze review dispositions (Codex Sol 6.1 xhigh, 2026-11-02, before the freeze commit)

Prompt: `freeze-review-prompt.md`. Raw output (full transcript incl. tool reads): `freeze-review-raw.txt`. Verdict: the stopped outcome is justified; freeze enforcement needed fixes. All 8 findings accepted and addressed before the freeze commit; no fresh seed had been read. A second review pass of the fixes was not run; the guard fixes are covered by stubbed-git tests instead.

| # | Sev | Finding | Disposition |
|---|-----|---------|-------------|
| 1 | P1 | guard read a local `origin/...` tracking ref, not the remote | Fixed. `freezeGuard` runs `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/<branch>`, takes the remote commit, requires it in the local object store, and compares that commit's blobs with `git hash-object`; stubbed tests for no remote branch, missing local commit, dirty file, missing or differing blob. |
| 2 | P1 | exported `generateFe()` bypassed the guard | Fixed. `generateFe(git)` calls `guarded()` first; `--write` and `--replay-check` reach simulation only through it; test: it rejects (stubbed git) before any simulation. |
| 3 | P2 | PLAN.md and Amendment 3 outside the lock | Fixed. `planSha256` in the lock, PLAN.md in `frozenPaths` (so also in the pushed-identical set), `verifyLock(planPath)` test that a document-only change fails. PLAN.md is therefore not edited after the freeze commit. |
| 4 | P2 | power wording overclaimed an exclusion bound; Holm threshold depends on candidate count | Fixed in `PROTOCOL_FE.powerStatement`, `LIMITS_FE`, the page disclosure and PLAN 4.2: a negative is a failure to detect under these rules; 13 of 16 with three candidates, 12 with one. |
| 5 | P2 | ladder stop rule ignored G-NOISE | Fixed. `settled()` (qualified, or passing dose known and all within-ceiling doses run); `runLadder(deltas, ceilings, ...)`; unit test with the reviewer's counterexample. Calibration re-run: byte-identical to the first run apart from the new field below. |
| 6 | P3 | fB `gap < 0` claim wrong (gap to b stayed positive in dev) | Fixed in code comment, PLAN 4.1 and the test (asserts `gap(fS) - gap(fB) = (eB - eA) * dB`). |
| 7 | P3 | `inoc2 <= inoc` by construction, so "did not rescue" was predetermined | Fixed. Added reported-only `inocMatched = Y(b) - max(fU, fS, fB)`; PLAN section 10 now says `inoc2` equalled `inoc` in 24 of 24 dev blocks and reports `inocMatched` (zero or negative in 21, positive in 3). The registered gate is unchanged. |
| 8 | P3 | wrong numeric summaries in Amendment 3 | Fixed: floor reductions 15.5%, 11.4%, 27.7%; best weakest-contrast median 1.5 (space contrast best 29.5); all recomputed from `calibration.json`. |

Reviewer confirmations kept: the lock matched all 38 sources, calibration, reservation digest and 176 enumerated seeds; saved sums, contrasts, deltas, ceilings and wall summaries agree; W2 reproduces zero placeable cells; the stopped report gives three `unsupported-measurement` labels and an empty negative list; free-E matching, signed-gap arithmetic, shared placement and audited conservation correct; physics packages, golden pins, `RULE_VERSION` and `defaultConfig` untouched; rung-1 `--check` passes.

# I06b evidence: free-energy-matched comparators, second rung (2026-11-02)

Machine-readable: `evidence.json` (sealed, seal 42ba2c6b3e935f32...), `manifest.json`, `calibration.json`, `protocol.lock.json`. Logs: `calibrate.log`, `write.log`, `checks.log`, `rung1-replay.log`. Freeze commit e7a48c06, pushed to `origin/claude/i06-free-energy-matched` before `--write` (the generator refuses otherwise). Exploratory, CPU only, mutation off.

## Result

**unsupported-measurement for all three candidates (clusters 0, 4, 9). Not a negative.** The ordinary-placement positive control found no detectable wall at any tested dose, so by the pre-stated rule no fresh builder block was simulated (fresh seeds 8601501-516 never read). This is the stated reason "none exists": the only legal geometry (a W1 ring) is not detectable at any dose in this assay, and the other geometry (W2) is not placeable under ordinary placement. This says nothing about walls the placement rule forbids.

- Placement: W2 (polymer floor under the receiver) is unavailable (0 common placeable cells); W1 (ring) is the only legal geometry.
- W1 dose ladder (8 wall seeds, four gating contrasts incl. inoc2, R = 4): 60, 120, 240 never reach 7 of 8 on all contrasts (best weakest-contrast median +1.5 at 240, above every ceiling); 600 and 1200 are worse than the comparators (weakest-contrast medians -172 and -194.5; at 1200 the limiting contrast is poly, -389 twice-median, while the builder contrast is -190). Ceilings (2 x builder polymer): 200, 204, 169.
- Noise floor delta (summed R = 4): 1152, 1031, 1169. Divided by R that is 288, 258, 292 against rung 1's 341, 291, 404 (floor lowered 15.5%, 11.4%, 27.7%): not enough, and the wall effect is far below even the new floor (G-NOISE needs the weakest median at least 2 x delta).
- Comparators: fU, fS, fB match the field arm's free energy exactly (9,394 for cluster 0 seed 8601001, against receiver-alone's 19,864 at equal total 25,624) with exact zero ledger residuals; they hold less total energy (constructionGap +10,470, +10,470, +10,380). `inoc2` negative in 24 of 24 dev blocks and equal to `inoc` in all 24 (its max is always set by the free-energy-rich receiver-alone controls d1E or d1B, so fU, fS and fB never enter the gating contrast on real fields). Plainly: the gating `inoc2` is still dominated by the controls that hold more free energy, so on these fields the new comparators change no gate outcome; only the reported `inocMatched` is free-energy-clean, and only the fixtures show the matched comparators changing a contrast. matched-only contrast `inocMatched` was zero or negative in 21 and positive in 3 (reported, not gating).
- far-field-energy fixture (8 seeds): naive 8/8 benefit; rung-1 inoculum contrast 8/8 positive (fooled); free-energy-matched `inoc2` 0/8 (caught); full gate rejects (poly 0/8). The claim is about the inoculum contrast only. Hand-wall W2 (allowP, frozen scale, diagnostic) passes the full gate: supported (7/8 on builder, poly, inoc2; 8/8 space), so the gate can say supported for a floor of 1200 polymer, which ordinary placement forbids.

## Correction recorded after review (2026-11-02)

The sealed `limits` text in `evidence.json` (and `LIMITS_FE` in the frozen `evidenceFe.ts`) says the positive control "shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose". That holds only if a control passes; in this run none did (every qualification false, every minimum detectable dose null). The sealed bytes are left as frozen; the lab page restates that sentence conditionally and states the failure (`limitsFeHtml`), and this section is the record. Rules, protocol and seals are unchanged.

## What this does and does not say

The assay at this scale cannot see a polymer structure of builder-like dose, so it cannot test whether a builder's field helps a receiver; it says nothing against niche construction. Next rung candidates (not run): a placement-legal control with real effect at small dose (e.g. protective polymer on cells the receiver may occupy via a different transfer rule), or a lower-variance readout than receiver biomass at T2.

    35	  });

    38	describe('niche fe freeze guard (git stubbed)', () => {
    39	  const paths = ['a.ts', 'b.ts'];
    40	  const git = (over: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {}): Git => (args) => {
    41	    if (args[0] === 'status') return over.status ?? '';
    42	    if (args[0] === 'ls-remote') { if (over.remote === 'throw') throw new Error('no such ref'); return over.remote ?? `${'a'.repeat(40)}\trefs/heads/${BRANCH}\n`; }
    43	    if (args[0] === 'ls-tree') { if (over.tree === 'throw') throw new Error('bad ref'); expect(args[2]).toBe('a'.repeat(40)); return over.tree ?? '100644 blob aaa\ta.ts\n100644 blob bbb\tb.ts\n'; }
    44	    if (args[0] === 'hash-object') return over.hash ?? 'aaa\nbbb\n';
    45	    throw new Error(args.join(' '));
    46	  };
    47	  it('allows an identical, clean, pushed freeze', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git())).toEqual([]));
    48	  it('refuses an uncommitted frozen file', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ status: ' M a.ts\n' })).join()).toMatch(/not committed clean/));
    49	  it('refuses when the remote has no such branch (the remote is asked, not a local tracking ref)', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ remote: 'throw' })).join()).toMatch(/push the freeze commit first/));
    50	  it('refuses when the remote commit is not available locally', () => expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ tree: 'throw' })).join()).toMatch(/git fetch/));
    51	  it('the generator itself refuses an unpublished or unverifiable freeze before simulating anything', async () => {
    52	    await expect(generateFe(git({ remote: 'throw' }))).rejects.toThrow(/refusing to read any fresh seed/);
    53	    await expect(generateFe(git({ status: ' M a.ts\n' }))).rejects.toThrow(/refusing to read any fresh seed/);
    54	  });
    55	  it('refuses a file absent from, or different on, the pushed branch', () => {
    56	    expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ tree: '100644 blob aaa\ta.ts\n' })).join()).toMatch(/b\.ts is not on/);
    57	    expect(freezeGuard(paths, git({ hash: 'aaa\nccc\n' })).join()).toMatch(/b\.ts differs from/);
    58	  });
    59	  it('the real freeze set names the lock, calibration, registry and every source', async () => {
    60	    const p = await frozenPaths();
    61	    expect(p).toContain('docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/PLAN.md');
    62	    for (const f of ['tools/niche/fe.ts', 'tools/niche/niche.ts', 'packages/runner/src/niche/freeEnergy.ts', 'packages/runner/src/niche/dose.ts', 'experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json', 'docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/protocol.lock.json', 'docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/calibration.json']) expect(p).toContain(f);
    63	    expect(BRANCH).toBe('claude/i06-free-energy-matched');
    64	  });
    65	  it.skipIf(!existsSync(LOCK_FE))('a document-only change to PLAN.md (the amendment) fails the lock', async () => {
    66	    const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'fe-plan-'));
    67	    try {
    68	      const copy = join(root, 'PLAN.md');
    69	      writeFileSync(copy, readFileSync(PLAN_FE, 'utf8') + '\nA later edit.\n');
    70	      expect((await verifyLock(copy)).join()).toMatch(/PLAN\.md/);
    71	    } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
    72	  });
    73	  it('--write and --replay-check refuse without a verifying lock', async () => {
    74	    if (!existsSync(LOCK_FE)) expect((await verifyLock()).join()).toMatch(/no protocol\.lock\.json/);
    75	    else expect(await verifyLock()).toEqual([]);
    76	  });
    77	});

     1	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     2	import { fieldBlock, fixtureProtocolFe, decideFixtureFe, freeGapSeed, nullBlockFe, runGuardsFe, takeoverBlockFe, wallGateBlockFe2 } from "../src/niche/fixturesFe.ts";
     3	import { runGuards } from "../src/niche/fixtures.ts";

     6	const fp = fixtureProtocolFe();
     7	const seeds = PROTOCOL_FE.fixtureSeeds;
     8	const delta = Math.ceil((fp.reserve.B * fp.R) / 50);

    10	describe("second-rung fixtures", () => {
    11	  it("far-field-energy: naive positive, rung-1 D_inoc positive (fooled), D_inoc2 not positive, full gate unsupported via D_poly = 0 (caughtBy recorded)", () => {
    12	    const r = decideFixtureFe("far-field-energy", seeds, seeds.map((s) => fieldBlock("far-field", s, fp)), delta);
    13	    expect(r.naiveWouldSay).toBe("benefit");
    14	    expect(r.inocFooledOld).toBe(true);
    15	    expect(r.inocCaughtNew).toBe(true);
    16	    expect(r.kNew).toBeLessThan(r.kOld);
    17	    expect(r.gatingK.poly).toBe(0);
    18	    expect(r.caughtBy).toContain("poly");
    19	    expect(r.caughtBy).toContain("inoc2");
    20	    expect(r.fullGateLabel).not.toBe("supported");
    21	    // d0 (and so d1E, d1B) hold less free energy than the field; fU, fS, fB hold the same.
    22	    const free = (id: string) => BigInt(r.freeBefore.find((x) => x.id === id)!.freeE);
    23	    expect(free("d0")).toBeLessThan(free("b"));
    24	    for (const id of ["fU", "fS", "fB"]) expect(free(id)).toBe(free("b"));
    25	  }, 120_000);
    26	
    27	  it("free-gap-replay closes the free-E gap: d0 holds more free E at equal totals, fU/fS/fB reproduce b's free E exactly, every residual 0n", () => {
    28	    for (const s of seeds) {
    29	      const r = freeGapSeed(s, fp);
    30	      expect(r.d0HoldsMoreFree).toBe(true);
    31	      expect(r.totalsEqual).toBe(true);
    32	      for (const id of ["fU", "fS", "fB"]) {
    33	        expect(r.freeDiffBefore[id]).toBe("0");
    34	        expect(r.freeDiffAfter[id]).toBe("0");
    35	      }
    36	      // The field here binds its energy as detritus B only: fU and fS hold less total energy, and fB (which is handed that biomass as inoculum) closes the total gap too.
    37	      for (const id of ["d0", "fU", "fS"]) expect(id === "d0" ? r.constructionGap[id] : BigInt(r.constructionGap[id]) > 0n).toBeTruthy();
    38	      expect(r.flags.fU).toBe("comparator-deficit");
    39	      expect(r.constructionGap.fB).toBe("0");
    40	      expect(r.flags.fB).toBe("total-matched");
    41	      expect(r.residualsZero).toBe(true);
    42	    }
    43	  });
    44	
    45	  it("takeover-only still has zero synthesis; null-field gives exact zero contrasts", () => {
    46	    const t = takeoverBlockFe(seeds[0], fp);
    47	    expect(t.outcomes.b.synthesis).toBe(0);
    48	    const n = nullBlockFe(seeds[0], fp);
    49	    expect(n.contrasts).toMatchObject({ builder: 0, poly: 0, space: 0, inoc2: 0, naive: 0 });
    50	  }, 60_000);
    51	
    52	  it("hand-wall-gate-W2 (rung-1 allowP wall at the frozen scale) is the positive control: all eight seeds, supported, no failed gating contrast", () => {
    53	    const p = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, R: PROTOCOL_FE.fixtureR };
    54	    expect(seeds).toHaveLength(8);
    55	    const r = decideFixtureFe("hand-wall-gate-W2", seeds, seeds.map((s) => wallGateBlockFe2(s, p)), Math.ceil((p.reserve.B * p.R) / 50));
    56	    expect(Object.keys(r.gatingK).sort()).toEqual(["builder", "inoc2", "poly", "space"]);
    57	    expect(r.fullGateLabel).toBe("supported");
    58	    expect(r.caughtBy).toEqual([]);
    59	    // 7 of 8 is the pass bar for each gating contrast (the 8-seed fixture exact-sign bar is the lab's frozen 7/8).
    60	    for (const c of ["builder", "poly", "space", "inoc2"]) expect(r.gatingK[c], c).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(7);
    61	  }, 600_000);

     1	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     2	import { canonicalJSON, stateHash } from "@bl/schema";
     3	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     4	import { buildArms, placeAll } from "../src/niche/arms.ts";
     5	import { runArm } from "../src/niche/assay.ts";
     6	import { ALPHA, signTestP } from "../src/niche/decision.ts";
     7	import { binomialUpperTail, decideCandidatesFe, panelVerdictFe, type CandidateInputFe } from "../src/niche/decisionFe.ts";
     8	import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, candidateOf, type ProtocolFe } from "../src/niche/protocol.ts";
     9	import { runFeArms, fixtureProtocolFe, detritusField } from "../src/niche/fixturesFe.ts";
    10	import { ARMS_FE, noiseBlock, runArmReplicated, runBlockFe, seedFor } from "../src/niche/replicate.ts";
    11	import { controlsAround } from "../src/niche/fixtures.ts";
    12	import { cloneAudit, cloneState } from "../src/niche/transfer.ts";
    13	
    14	const short: ProtocolFe = { ...PROTOCOL_FE, T1: 8, T2: 12, census: 1, R: 2 };
    15	const placed = (p = short) => {
    16	  const b = buildArms(candidateOf(0, p), p.devSeeds[1], p);
    17	  placeAll(b.arms, p);
    18	  return b;
    19	};
    20	
    21	describe("replicated readout", () => {
    22	  it("replicate offsets are identical in every arm", () => {
    23	    const b = placed();
    24	    const seen: Record<string, Set<number>> = {};
    25	    for (const id of ["b", "nb", "d0"]) {
    26	      seen[id] = new Set();
    27	      runArmReplicated(b.arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2, { onStep: (s) => void seen[id].add(s.cfg.seed) });
    28	    }
    29	    const want = [seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 0), seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1)];
    30	    for (const id of Object.keys(seen)) expect([...seen[id]].sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual(want);
    31	  });
    32	
    33	  it("Y is the integer sum of the R replicate endpoints; AUC is reported and never gating", () => {
    34	    const b = placed();
    35	    const o = runArmReplicated(b.arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2);
    36	    expect(o.ys).toHaveLength(2);
    37	    expect(o.y).toBe(o.ys[0] + o.ys[1]);
    38	    expect(Number.isInteger(o.y) && Number.isInteger(o.auc)).toBe(true);
    39	    expect(o.auc).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    40	    expect(GATING_FE as readonly string[]).not.toContain("auc");
    41	  });
    42	
    43	  it("replicate 0 is the rung-1 pipeline at the block seed", () => {
    44	    const b = placed();
    45	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    46	    const plain = runArm({ ...x, state: cloneState(x.state), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit) }, short);
    47	    expect(runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 1).endHashes[0]).toBe(plain.endHash);
    48	  });
    49	
    50	  it("instrumented twin equals plain twin (stateHash) per replicate", () => {
    51	    const b = placed();
    52	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    53	    const plain = runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2);
    54	    let calls = 0;
    55	    const inst = runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2, { onStep: () => void calls++ });
    56	    expect(inst.endHashes).toEqual(plain.endHashes);
    57	    expect(calls).toBe(2 * short.T2);
    58	    const twin = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1) }));
    59	    twin.run(short.T2);
    60	    expect(plain.endHashes[1]).toBe(stateHash(twin.state));
    61	  });
    62	
    63	  it("deterministic replay: the same block twice gives equal bytes; every census of every replicate audits to zero", () => {
    64	    const a = runBlockFe(0, short.devSeeds[1], short), b = runBlockFe(0, short.devSeeds[1], short);
    65	    expect(a.valid).toBe(true);
    66	    expect(canonicalJSON(a)).toBe(canonicalJSON(b));
    67	    expect(Object.keys(a.outcomes).sort()).toEqual([...ARMS_FE].sort());
    68	    for (const recs of Object.values(a.transfers)) for (const r of recs) expect([r.residual, r.matterResidual]).toEqual(["0", "0"]);
    69	  }, 60_000);
    70	
    71	  it("segmented stage 2 equals continuous at a replicate seed (checkpoint-free split of the same run)", () => {
    72	    const b = placed();
    73	    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    74	    const cfg = { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1) };
    75	    const cont = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, cfg));
    76	    cont.run(short.T2);
    77	    const half = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, cfg));
    78	    half.run(short.T2 / 2);
    79	    half.run(short.T2 / 2);
    80	    expect(stateHash(half.state)).toBe(stateHash(cont.state));
    81	  });
    82	
    83	  it("R=4 sum noise floor on a no-op control is below the R=1 floor scaled by R (dev-scale fixture with injury)", () => {
    84	    const fp = { ...fixtureProtocolFe(), R: 4, injury: { period: 6, radius: 2, prob: 40_000_000, shell: 15 } };
    85	    let f1 = 0, f4 = 0;
    86	    for (const s of PROTOCOL_FE.devReplicateSeeds) {
    87	      const { base, cond } = detritusField(s, fp);
    88	      const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, s, fp);
    89	      placeAll(arms, fp);
    90	      const b = arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!;
    91	      const A = [0, 1, 2, 3], B = [4, 5, 6, 7];
    92	      const y = (offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(b, s, fp, 4, {}, offs);
    93	      const ya = y(A), yb = y(B);
    94	      f4 = Math.max(f4, Math.abs(ya.y - yb.y));
    95	      f1 = Math.max(f1, Math.abs(ya.ys[0] - yb.ys[0]));
    96	    }
    97	    // The R = 4 floor must be strictly below R = 1 scaled by R, and the control must not be degenerate (replay: f1 = 231, f4 = 500 < 924).
    98	    expect(f1).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    99	    expect(f4).toBeLessThan(4 * f1);
   100	  }, 120_000);
   101	
   102	  it("block viability is the R-1-of-R conversion of the field arm's surviving replicates (R = 4)", () => {
   103	    const p4: ProtocolFe = { ...short, R: 4 };
   104	    // The conversion lives inside runBlockFe (a frozen source), so it is checked there: viable must equal survivedReps >= R - 1 for each block,
   105	    // and the survivedReps must be the count of positive replicate endpoints. Hand table for the rule itself:
   106	    const rule = (surv: number, R: number) => surv >= R - 1;
   107	    expect([2, 3, 4].map((k) => rule(k, 4))).toEqual([false, true, true]);
   108	    for (const s of [PROTOCOL_FE.devSeeds[0], PROTOCOL_FE.devSeeds[1]]) {
   109	      const r = runBlockFe(PROTOCOL_FE.panel[0], s, p4);
   110	      if (!r.valid) continue;
   111	      expect(r.outcomes.b.survivedReps).toBe(r.outcomes.b.ys.filter((y) => y > 0).length);
   112	      expect(r.viable).toBe(rule(r.outcomes.b.survivedReps, 4));
   113	    }
   114	  }, 300_000);
   115	
   116	  it("noiseBlock refuses an R that would leave the reserved replicate offsets", () => {
   117	    expect(() => noiseBlock(0, PROTOCOL_FE.devReplicateSeeds[0], { ...PROTOCOL_FE, R: 8 })).toThrow(/reserved/);
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  it("runFeArms returns the second-rung contrasts with inoc2 at most inoc", () => {
   121	    const fp = fixtureProtocolFe();
   122	    const { base, cond } = detritusField(2, fp);
   123	    const r = runFeArms(cond, base, 2, fp);
   124	    expect(r.contrasts.inoc2).toBeLessThanOrEqual(r.contrasts.inoc);
   125	    expect(r.outcomes.fU).toBeDefined();
   126	  });
   127	});

   129	const inputOf = (id: string, k: number, n = 16, over: Partial<CandidateInputFe> = {}): CandidateInputFe => {
   130	  const d = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => (i < k ? 100 : -100));
   131	  return { id, status: "ok", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: d, poly: d, space: d, inoc2: d, spaceP: d, spaceNP: d, inoc: d, inocMatched: d, naive: d }, delta: 10, viableBlocks: 16, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [], ...over };
   132	};
   133	
   134	describe("second-rung decision", () => {
   135	  it("exact sign p for n=16 at k=12, 14, 16 against hand enumeration", () => {
   136	    expect(signTestP(16, 16)).toEqual({ num: 1, den: 65536 });
   137	    expect(signTestP(16, 14)).toEqual({ num: 120 + 16 + 1, den: 65536 });
   138	    expect(signTestP(16, 12)).toEqual({ num: 1820 + 560 + 120 + 16 + 1 - 0 - 0, den: 65536 });
   139	    expect(signTestP(16, 13)).toEqual({ num: 697, den: 65536 });
   140	  });
   141	
   142	  it("Holm resolution at n=16 over three candidates: thresholds 1/60, 1/40, 1/20 and k >= 13 passes the first", () => {
   143	    const rs = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16), inputOf("c4", 16), inputOf("c9", 13)]);
   144	    expect(rs.map((r) => r.label)).toEqual(["supported", "supported", "supported"]);
   145	    expect(rs.map((r) => r.holmThreshold!.den).sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual([20, 40, 60]);
   146	    // k = 12 (p = 2517/65536) still clears the last Holm step (1/20) behind two perfect candidates; k = 11 (6885/65536) does not.
   147	    expect(decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 12), inputOf("c4", 16), inputOf("c9", 16)]).find((r) => r.id === "c0")!.label).toBe("supported");
   148	    const bad = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 11), inputOf("c4", 16), inputOf("c9", 16)]);
   149	    expect(bad.find((r) => r.id === "c0")!.label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
   150	  });
   151	
   152	  it("no floats in the sealed payload shape; power table values are exact (0.75: 40.5%, 0.9: 93.2%)", () => {
   153	    expect(() => canonicalJSON(decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16)]))).not.toThrow();
   154	    const pct = (qn: bigint, qd: bigint) => {
   155	      const t = binomialUpperTail(16, 13, qn, qd);
   156	      return Number((t.num * 10000n) / t.den) / 100;
   157	    };
   158	    expect(pct(3n, 4n)).toBeCloseTo(40.49, 1);
   159	    expect(pct(17n, 20n)).toBeCloseTo(78.98, 1);
   160	    expect(pct(9n, 10n)).toBeCloseTo(93.15, 1);
   161	    expect(pct(19n, 20n)).toBeCloseTo(99.29, 1);
   162	    expect(binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).num * 65536n).toBe(697n * binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).den);
   163	    expect(PROTOCOL_FE.powerStatement).toMatch(/40\.5%.*79%.*93%.*99%/);
   164	    expect(ALPHA).toEqual({ num: 1, den: 20 });
   165	  });
   166	
   167	  it("field arm viability requires 14 of 16 blocks; the paired survival contrast is not gating", () => {
   168	    const ok = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16, 16, { viableBlocks: 14 })])[0];
   169	    const no = decideCandidatesFe([inputOf("c0", 16, 16, { viableBlocks: 13 })])[0];
   170	    expect(ok.label).toBe("supported");
   171	    expect(no.label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
   172	    expect(no.reasons.join(" ")).toMatch(/viable/);
   173	    expect(GATING_FE as readonly string[]).not.toContain("survival");
   174	  });
   175	
   176	  it("a candidate the positive control did not qualify is unsupported-measurement, never a negative; the panel is not negative", () => {
   177	    const ns = inputOf("c0", 0, 16, { status: "not-simulated", notSimulatedReasons: ["minimum detectable dose 600 exceeds the ceiling 170"] });
   178	    const rs = decideCandidatesFe([ns, inputOf("c4", 0), { ...ns, id: "c9" }]);
   179	    expect(rs.find((r) => r.id === "c0")).toMatchObject({ label: "unsupported-measurement", simulated: false });
   180	    expect(rs.find((r) => r.id === "c4")!.label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
   181	    expect(panelVerdictFe(rs).verdict).toBe("unsupported-measurement");
   182	    expect(panelVerdictFe([rs[1]]).verdict).toBe("no-causal-benefit-detected");
   183	  });
   184	});

     1	// Pure helpers for the second-rung section of the niche page (free-energy-matched comparators, noise vs effect, dose ladder, edge per
     2	// candidate, far-field-energy panel). Reads the sealed second-rung evidence only and runs no simulation.
     3	import { edgeOf, esc, fmt, half, ratText, type Rational } from './render.ts';
     4	
     5	interface FreeRowView { id: string; freeE: string; total: string; constructionGap: string; flag: string }
     6	interface BlockFeView { seed: number; valid?: boolean; freeBefore?: FreeRowView[]; freeAfter?: FreeRowView[]; invalidReason?: string | null }
     7	interface DoseStat { k: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number }
     8	interface DoseRowView { totalP: number; seeds: readonly number[]; stats: Record<string, DoseStat>; limiting: string; limitingMedianTimes2: number }
     9	interface QualView { cluster: number; delta: number; ceiling: number; minDetectableDose: number | null; qualifyingDose: number | null; qualified: boolean; reason: string }
    10	interface ResultFeView {
    11	  id: string; label: string; simulated: boolean; mechanism: string; reasons: string[]; pCand: Rational; holmThreshold: Rational | null;
    12	  gating: Record<string, { k: number; n: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number; passes: boolean }>;
    13	  reported: { inoc: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number }; naive: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number } };
    14	}
    15	interface FixtureFeView { id: string; seeds: readonly number[]; naiveK: number; naiveWouldSay: string; inocFooledOld: boolean; inocCaughtNew: boolean; kOld: number; kNew: number; gatingK: Record<string, number>; caughtBy: string[]; fullGateLabel: string; freeBefore: FreeRowView[] }
    16	export interface EvidenceFeView {
    17	  protocolHash: string; integritySha256: string; amendments?: string[]; powerStatement: string; limits: string[];
    18	  protocol: { R: number; freshSeeds: readonly number[]; wallSeeds: readonly number[] };
    19	  dev: { R: number; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; deltaProposed: number; builderPMedianTimes2: number }[]; ladder: { rows: DoseRowView[]; w2: { status: string; reason: string } }; qualification: QualView[] };
    20	  fresh: { ran: boolean; seeds: readonly number[]; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>; survivalPositiveBlocks: number; input: { viableBlocks: number; expectedBlocks: number } }[]; results: ResultFeView[]; panel: { verdict: string; text: string }; caveats: string[] };
    21	  fixtures: { reports: FixtureFeView[]; freeGap: { seed: number; d0FreeE: string; bFreeE: string; d0Total: string; bTotal: string }[]; guards: { id: string; ok: boolean }[] };
    22	}
    23	
    24	export const COMPARATOR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    25	  b: 'Builder removed (the field arm)',
    26	  d0: 'Receiver alone, total energy matched',
    27	  d1E: 'Receiver alone + field energy near the inoculum',
    28	  d1B: 'Receiver alone + field matter near the inoculum',
    29	  fU: 'Free-energy matched, spread uniformly',
    30	  fS: 'Free-energy matched, same pattern cell for cell',
    31	  fB: 'Free-energy matched + the field\'s biomass',
    32	};
    33	const FREE_ORDER = ['b', 'd0', 'd1E', 'd1B', 'fU', 'fS', 'fB'];
    34	const GAP_WORD: Record<string, string> = { 'comparator-deficit': 'holds less total energy than the field', 'comparator-excess': 'holds more total energy than the field', 'total-matched': 'total energy equal', reference: 'reference' };
    35	
    36	/** Free E and total energy per comparator for one block: the d0-versus-b gap (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style numbers) and what matching does to it. */
    37	export function freeEnergyHtml(rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined, seed: number | undefined): string {
    38	  if (!rows?.length) return '<tr><td colspan="4">No block carries a free-energy table.</td></tr>';
    39	  const by = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
    40	  return FREE_ORDER.filter((id) => by.has(id)).map((id) => {
    41	    const r = by.get(id)!;
    42	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(COMPARATOR_TEXT[id] ?? id)}</th><td>${fmt(Number(r.freeE))}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.total))}</td><td>${r.id === 'b' ? 'reference' : `${esc(fmt(Number(r.constructionGap)))}: ${esc(GAP_WORD[r.flag] ?? r.flag)}`}</td></tr>`;
    43	  }).join('') + (seed === undefined ? '' : `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">Block seed ${seed}, before the inoculum is placed. Gap = total(field arm) minus total(comparator).</td></tr>`);
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function noiseEffectHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    47	  const rows = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => {
    48	    const row = ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.qualifyingDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.minDetectableDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.reduce<DoseRowView | undefined>((m, r) => (!m || r.limitingMedianTimes2 > m.limitingMedianTimes2 ? r : m), undefined);
    49	    const effect = row ? row.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2 : null;
    50	    const max = Math.max(1, q.delta, effect ?? 0);
    51	    const bar = (v: number | null, cls: string) => `<span class="${cls}" style="width:${Math.round((100 * Math.max(0, v ?? 0)) / max)}%"></span>`;
    52	    return `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.delta)}</td><td>${effect === null ? 'n/a' : half(row!.limitingMedianTimes2)}${row ? ` at dose ${fmt(row.totalP)} (${esc(row.limiting)}); ${q.qualified || q.minDetectableDose !== null ? 'wall detected' : 'wall not detected at any dose, best dose shown'}` : ''}</td>
    53	      <td><div class="bar" role="img" aria-label="noise floor delta ${fmt(q.delta)} against the wall effect ${effect === null ? 'not detected' : fmt(effect)}">${bar(q.delta, 'bar-noise')}</div><div class="bar">${bar(effect, 'bar-effect')}</div></td></tr>`;
    54	  });
    55	  return rows.join('');
    56	}
    57	
    58	export function doseLadderHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    59	  const n = ev.protocol.wallSeeds.length;
    60	  const rows = ev.dev.ladder.rows.map((r) => {
    61	    const gk = Object.entries(r.stats).map(([k, s]) => `${esc(k)} ${s.k}/${n}`).join(', ');
    62	    const passes = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `c${q.cluster}: ${r.totalP <= q.ceiling ? 'within ceiling' : 'above ceiling'}`).join('; ');
    63	    return `<tr><th scope="row">W1 ring, polymer ${fmt(r.totalP)}</th><td>${gk}</td><td>${esc(r.limiting)} ${half(r.limitingMedianTimes2)}</td><td>${esc(passes)}</td></tr>`;
    64	  });
    65	  rows.push(`<tr><th scope="row">W2 floor under the receiver</th><td colspan="3">${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.status)}: ${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.reason)}</td></tr>`);
    66	  return rows.join('');
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function qualificationHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    70	  return ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.ceiling)}</td><td>${q.minDetectableDose === null ? 'none on the ladder' : fmt(q.minDetectableDose)}</td><td>${q.qualified ? `qualified at ${fmt(q.qualifyingDose)}` : 'not qualified'}</td><td>${esc(q.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    71	}
    72	
    73	export function edgeFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    74	  return ev.fresh.results.map((r) => {
    75	    const e = edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism);
    76	    const g = ['builder', 'poly', 'space', 'inoc2'].map((k) => (r.simulated ? `${esc(k)} ${r.gating[k].k}/${r.gating[k].n}${r.gating[k].passes ? '' : ' (no)'}` : `${esc(k)} not run`)).join(', ');
    77	    const word = r.simulated ? e.text : 'Not simulated: the positive control did not qualify this builder, so there is no negative and no positive here.';
    78	    return `<li class="edge-fe edge-${e.state}"><strong>${esc(r.id.replace('cluster-', 'Builder cluster '))}: edge ${esc(e.word)}.</strong> ${esc(word)}<br><span class="muted">Label <code>${esc(r.label)}</code>${r.reasons.length ? ` (${esc(r.reasons.join('; '))})` : ''}. Blocks positive: ${g}.</span></li>`;
    79	  }).join('');
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function farFieldHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    83	  const rows = ev.fixtures.reports.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${esc(f.naiveWouldSay)} (${f.naiveK} of ${f.seeds.length})</td><td>${f.kOld} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocFooledOld ? 'fooled' : 'not fooled'}</td><td>${f.kNew} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocCaughtNew ? 'caught' : 'not caught'}</td><td>${esc(f.fullGateLabel)}${f.caughtBy.length ? ` (rejected by ${esc(f.caughtBy.join(', '))})` : ''}</td></tr>`);
    84	  return rows.join('');
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function verdictFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    88	  const f = ev.fresh;
    89	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No fresh block was simulated, so there are no per-candidate contrasts: this states how sensitive the assay is at realistic polymer doses, not that no niche is built.</p>';
    90	  return `<p class="verdict-text"><strong>${esc(f.panel.verdict.replaceAll('-', ' '))}.</strong> ${esc(f.panel.text)}</p>${stopped}
    91	    <ul>${f.caveats.map((c) => `<li>${esc(c)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    92	    <p class="muted">Protocol hash <code>${esc(ev.protocolHash.slice(0, 16))}</code>, seal <code>${esc(ev.integritySha256.slice(0, 16))}</code>. Each arm summed over ${ev.protocol.R} replicate sims; ${f.ran ? `${f.seeds.length} fresh blocks per qualified candidate` : 'no fresh blocks'}; ${ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks.length ?? 0} dev blocks per candidate were used for calibration only.</p>`;
    93	}
    94	
    95	const SHOWS_CLAIM = 'It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder\'s field can pass.';
    96	
    97	/**
    98	 * The limits list. The sealed text of the positive-control limit says the control "shows the assay can detect" a polymer structure; that holds
    99	 * only when a control passes. The sealed evidence is frozen, so the page restates that sentence conditionally and, when no candidate was
   100	 * qualified, says plainly that this run's control failed. (Correction recorded in docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/REVIEW.md.)
   101	 */
   102	export function limitsFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
   103	  const none = ev.dev.qualification.every((q) => !q.qualified);
   104	  return ev.limits.map((l) => {
   105	    if (!l.includes(SHOWS_CLAIM)) return `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`;
   106	    const fixed = l.replace(SHOWS_CLAIM, `A passing control would demonstrate that the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass.${none ? ' In this run no control passed at any dose: every qualification is false and no minimum detectable dose exists, so this run shows the assay could not detect a polymer ring of any tested dose.' : ''}`);
   107	    return `<li>${esc(fixed)}</li>`;
   108	  }).join('');
   109	}
   110	
   111	export function pendingHtml(): string {
   112	  return '<p class="muted">The second-rung evidence has not been written yet (docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json). Run <code>node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --write</code> after the freeze.</p>';
   113	}

   115	/** First block that carries a free-energy table, from the fresh blocks if any ran, otherwise dev. */
   116	export function firstFreeBlock(ev: EvidenceFeView): { rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined; seed: number | undefined } {
   117	  const b = ev.fresh.candidates[0]?.blocks[0] ?? ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks[0];
   118	  return { rows: b?.freeBefore, seed: b?.seed };
   119	}

    10	function initSecondRung() {
    11	  if (!fe) { el('r2-verdict').innerHTML = pendingHtml(); return; }
    12	  const { rows, seed } = firstFreeBlock(fe);
    13	  el('r2-verdict').innerHTML = verdictFeHtml(fe);
    14	  el('r2-edges').innerHTML = edgeFeHtml(fe);
    15	  el('r2-free').innerHTML = freeEnergyHtml(rows, seed);
    16	  el('r2-noise').innerHTML = noiseEffectHtml(fe);
    17	  el('r2-ladder').innerHTML = doseLadderHtml(fe);
    18	  el('r2-qual').innerHTML = qualificationHtml(fe);
    19	  el('r2-far').innerHTML = farFieldHtml(fe);
    20	  el('r2-limits').innerHTML = limitsFeHtml(fe);
    21	}

    23	const ev = evidence as unknown as EvidenceView;
    24	const el = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;
    25	const sel = el<HTMLSelectElement>('cand');
    26	
    27	function render() {
    28	  const cluster = sel.value === '' ? undefined : Number(sel.value);
    29	  const c = ev.fresh.candidates.find((x) => x.cluster === cluster);
    30	  const r = ev.fresh.results.find((x) => x.id === `cluster-${cluster}`);
    31	  el('edge').innerHTML = edgeHtml(ev, cluster);
    32	  el('arms').innerHTML = armsHtml(c);
    33	  el('contrasts').innerHTML = contrastsHtml(r);
    34	  el('ledger').innerHTML = ledgerHtml(c);
    35	  el('ledger-note').textContent = ledgerNote(ev, c);
    36	  el('cost').innerHTML = costHtml(c);
    37	  el('flux').textContent = fluxText(c);
    38	}
    39	
    40	function init() {
    41	  sel.innerHTML = candidateOptions(ev);
    42	  sel.disabled = ev.fresh.candidates.length === 0;
    43	  sel.addEventListener('change', render);
    44	  el('limits').innerHTML = ev.limits.map((l) => `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`).join('');
    45	  el('verdict').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', verdictHtml(ev));
    46	  el('fixtures').innerHTML = fixturesHtml(ev);
    47	  el('guards').textContent = guardsText(ev);
    48	  el('wall').innerHTML = wallHtml(ev);
    49	  el('devrows').innerHTML = devRowsHtml(ev);
    50	  render();
    51	  initSecondRung();
    52	}

    13	  <main id="main">
    14	    <section class="intro">
    15	      <p class="eyebrow">First causal rung</p>
    16	      <h1>Does what a builder leaves behind help a receiver?</h1>
    17	      <p>A builder lineage conditions a small world: it lays down polymer and reshapes the nutrient, waste and energy around it. Then the builder is taken away, a receiver is placed on empty ground, and we ask whether the leftovers, and not something else, help it. Every transfer moves matter and energy by exact accounting, and the same receiver is also run against copies of the world that keep the totals but lose the structure, or give the receiver the energy alone.</p>
    18	      <p class="disclosure">Exploratory, CPU only, no GPU evidence, mutation off. A local experiment, kept off the public site. A negative here means "no causal benefit detected in this small panel, horizon and field", not that a second link is impossible.</p>
    19	    </section>
    20	
    21	    <section class="rung2" id="rung2" aria-labelledby="r2-heading">
    22	      <p class="eyebrow">Second rung: free-energy-matched</p>
    23	      <h2 id="r2-heading">Same question, with fairer comparators and a better-powered readout</h2>
    24	      <p>The first rung could not tell whether a negative came from the field or from the receiver-alone controls holding more free energy (for one block, 22,169 against 9,917, at equal total energy). This rung adds comparators that match the field's free energy exactly, sums each arm over several replicate runs to push the noise floor down, and asks whether a hand-built polymer wall of a realistic dose can be seen at all before any fresh builder block is run.</p>
    25	      <p class="disclosure">Exploratory, CPU only, no GPU evidence, mutation off. Local, kept off the public site. A negative would only mean a consistent benefit was not detected under these rules, not that there is no benefit and not that a second link is impossible.</p>

     1	// Pure helpers for the second-rung section of the niche page (free-energy-matched comparators, noise vs effect, dose ladder, edge per
     2	// candidate, far-field-energy panel). Reads the sealed second-rung evidence only and runs no simulation.
     3	import { edgeOf, esc, fmt, half, ratText, type Rational } from './render.ts';
     4	
     5	interface FreeRowView { id: string; freeE: string; total: string; constructionGap: string; flag: string }
     6	interface BlockFeView { seed: number; valid?: boolean; freeBefore?: FreeRowView[]; freeAfter?: FreeRowView[]; invalidReason?: string | null }
     7	interface DoseStat { k: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number }
     8	interface DoseRowView { totalP: number; seeds: readonly number[]; stats: Record<string, DoseStat>; limiting: string; limitingMedianTimes2: number }
     9	interface QualView { cluster: number; delta: number; ceiling: number; minDetectableDose: number | null; qualifyingDose: number | null; qualified: boolean; reason: string }
    10	interface ResultFeView {
    11	  id: string; label: string; simulated: boolean; mechanism: string; reasons: string[]; pCand: Rational; holmThreshold: Rational | null;
    12	  gating: Record<string, { k: number; n: number; p: Rational; medianTimes2: number; passes: boolean }>;
    13	  reported: { inoc: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number }; naive: { k: number; n: number; medianTimes2: number } };
    14	}
    15	interface FixtureFeView { id: string; seeds: readonly number[]; naiveK: number; naiveWouldSay: string; inocFooledOld: boolean; inocCaughtNew: boolean; kOld: number; kNew: number; gatingK: Record<string, number>; caughtBy: string[]; fullGateLabel: string; freeBefore: FreeRowView[] }
    16	export interface EvidenceFeView {
    17	  protocolHash: string; integritySha256: string; amendments?: string[]; powerStatement: string; limits: string[];
    18	  protocol: { R: number; freshSeeds: readonly number[]; wallSeeds: readonly number[] };
    19	  dev: { R: number; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; deltaProposed: number; builderPMedianTimes2: number }[]; ladder: { rows: DoseRowView[]; w2: { status: string; reason: string } }; qualification: QualView[] };
    20	  fresh: { ran: boolean; seeds: readonly number[]; candidates: { cluster: number; blocks: BlockFeView[]; medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>; survivalPositiveBlocks: number; input: { viableBlocks: number; expectedBlocks: number } }[]; results: ResultFeView[]; panel: { verdict: string; text: string }; caveats: string[] };
    21	  fixtures: { reports: FixtureFeView[]; freeGap: { seed: number; d0FreeE: string; bFreeE: string; d0Total: string; bTotal: string }[]; guards: { id: string; ok: boolean }[] };

    24	export const COMPARATOR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    25	  b: 'Builder removed (the field arm)',
    26	  d0: 'Receiver alone, total energy matched',
    27	  d1E: 'Receiver alone + field energy near the inoculum',
    28	  d1B: 'Receiver alone + field matter near the inoculum',
    29	  fU: 'Free-energy matched, spread uniformly',
    30	  fS: 'Free-energy matched, same pattern cell for cell',
    31	  fB: 'Free-energy matched + the field\'s biomass',
    32	};
    33	const FREE_ORDER = ['b', 'd0', 'd1E', 'd1B', 'fU', 'fS', 'fB'];
    34	const GAP_WORD: Record<string, string> = { 'comparator-deficit': 'holds less total energy than the field', 'comparator-excess': 'holds more total energy than the field', 'total-matched': 'total energy equal', reference: 'reference' };
    35	
    36	/** Free E and total energy per comparator for one block: the d0-versus-b gap (the 9,917 vs 22,169 style numbers) and what matching does to it. */
    37	export function freeEnergyHtml(rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined, seed: number | undefined): string {
    38	  if (!rows?.length) return '<tr><td colspan="4">No block carries a free-energy table.</td></tr>';
    39	  const by = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
    40	  return FREE_ORDER.filter((id) => by.has(id)).map((id) => {
    41	    const r = by.get(id)!;
    42	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(COMPARATOR_TEXT[id] ?? id)}</th><td>${fmt(Number(r.freeE))}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.total))}</td><td>${r.id === 'b' ? 'reference' : `${esc(fmt(Number(r.constructionGap)))}: ${esc(GAP_WORD[r.flag] ?? r.flag)}`}</td></tr>`;
    43	  }).join('') + (seed === undefined ? '' : `<tr><td colspan="4" class="muted">Block seed ${seed}, before the inoculum is placed. Gap = total(field arm) minus total(comparator).</td></tr>`);
    44	}

    46	export function noiseEffectHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    47	  const rows = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => {
    48	    const row = ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.qualifyingDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.find((r) => r.totalP === q.minDetectableDose) ?? ev.dev.ladder.rows.reduce<DoseRowView | undefined>((m, r) => (!m || r.limitingMedianTimes2 > m.limitingMedianTimes2 ? r : m), undefined);
    49	    const effect = row ? row.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2 : null;
    50	    const max = Math.max(1, q.delta, effect ?? 0);
    51	    const bar = (v: number | null, cls: string) => `<span class="${cls}" style="width:${Math.round((100 * Math.max(0, v ?? 0)) / max)}%"></span>`;
    52	    return `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.delta)}</td><td>${effect === null ? 'n/a' : half(row!.limitingMedianTimes2)}${row ? ` at dose ${fmt(row.totalP)} (${esc(row.limiting)}); ${q.qualified || q.minDetectableDose !== null ? 'wall detected' : 'wall not detected at any dose, best dose shown'}` : ''}</td>
    53	      <td><div class="bar" role="img" aria-label="noise floor delta ${fmt(q.delta)} against the wall effect ${effect === null ? 'not detected' : fmt(effect)}">${bar(q.delta, 'bar-noise')}</div><div class="bar">${bar(effect, 'bar-effect')}</div></td></tr>`;
    54	  });
    55	  return rows.join('');
    56	}
    57	
    58	export function doseLadderHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    59	  const n = ev.protocol.wallSeeds.length;
    60	  const rows = ev.dev.ladder.rows.map((r) => {
    61	    const gk = Object.entries(r.stats).map(([k, s]) => `${esc(k)} ${s.k}/${n}`).join(', ');
    62	    const passes = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `c${q.cluster}: ${r.totalP <= q.ceiling ? 'within ceiling' : 'above ceiling'}`).join('; ');
    63	    return `<tr><th scope="row">W1 ring, polymer ${fmt(r.totalP)}</th><td>${gk}</td><td>${esc(r.limiting)} ${half(r.limitingMedianTimes2)}</td><td>${esc(passes)}</td></tr>`;
    64	  });
    65	  rows.push(`<tr><th scope="row">W2 floor under the receiver</th><td colspan="3">${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.status)}: ${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.reason)}</td></tr>`);
    66	  return rows.join('');
    67	}

    69	export function qualificationHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    70	  return ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.ceiling)}</td><td>${q.minDetectableDose === null ? 'none on the ladder' : fmt(q.minDetectableDose)}</td><td>${q.qualified ? `qualified at ${fmt(q.qualifyingDose)}` : 'not qualified'}</td><td>${esc(q.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    71	}

    73	export function edgeFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    74	  return ev.fresh.results.map((r) => {
    75	    const e = edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism);
    76	    const g = ['builder', 'poly', 'space', 'inoc2'].map((k) => (r.simulated ? `${esc(k)} ${r.gating[k].k}/${r.gating[k].n}${r.gating[k].passes ? '' : ' (no)'}` : `${esc(k)} not run`)).join(', ');
    77	    const word = r.simulated ? e.text : 'Not simulated: the positive control did not qualify this builder, so there is no negative and no positive here.';
    78	    return `<li class="edge-fe edge-${e.state}"><strong>${esc(r.id.replace('cluster-', 'Builder cluster '))}: edge ${esc(e.word)}.</strong> ${esc(word)}<br><span class="muted">Label <code>${esc(r.label)}</code>${r.reasons.length ? ` (${esc(r.reasons.join('; '))})` : ''}. Blocks positive: ${g}.</span></li>`;
    79	  }).join('');
    80	}

    82	export function farFieldHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    83	  const rows = ev.fixtures.reports.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${esc(f.naiveWouldSay)} (${f.naiveK} of ${f.seeds.length})</td><td>${f.kOld} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocFooledOld ? 'fooled' : 'not fooled'}</td><td>${f.kNew} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocCaughtNew ? 'caught' : 'not caught'}</td><td>${esc(f.fullGateLabel)}${f.caughtBy.length ? ` (rejected by ${esc(f.caughtBy.join(', '))})` : ''}</td></tr>`);
    84	  return rows.join('');
    85	}

    87	export function verdictFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    88	  const f = ev.fresh;
    89	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No fresh block was simulated, so there are no per-candidate contrasts: this states how sensitive the assay is at realistic polymer doses, not that no niche is built.</p>';
    90	  return `<p class="verdict-text"><strong>${esc(f.panel.verdict.replaceAll('-', ' '))}.</strong> ${esc(f.panel.text)}</p>${stopped}
    91	    <ul>${f.caveats.map((c) => `<li>${esc(c)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    92	    <p class="muted">Protocol hash <code>${esc(ev.protocolHash.slice(0, 16))}</code>, seal <code>${esc(ev.integritySha256.slice(0, 16))}</code>. Each arm summed over ${ev.protocol.R} replicate sims; ${f.ran ? `${f.seeds.length} fresh blocks per qualified candidate` : 'no fresh blocks'}; ${ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks.length ?? 0} dev blocks per candidate were used for calibration only.</p>`;
    93	}

    95	const SHOWS_CLAIM = 'It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder\'s field can pass.';
    96	
    97	/**
    98	 * The limits list. The sealed text of the positive-control limit says the control "shows the assay can detect" a polymer structure; that holds
    99	 * only when a control passes. The sealed evidence is frozen, so the page restates that sentence conditionally and, when no candidate was
   100	 * qualified, says plainly that this run's control failed. (Correction recorded in docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/REVIEW.md.)
   101	 */
   102	export function limitsFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
   103	  const none = ev.dev.qualification.every((q) => !q.qualified);
   104	  return ev.limits.map((l) => {
   105	    if (!l.includes(SHOWS_CLAIM)) return `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`;
   106	    const fixed = l.replace(SHOWS_CLAIM, `A passing control would demonstrate that the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass.${none ? ' In this run no control passed at any dose: every qualification is false and no minimum detectable dose exists, so this run shows the assay could not detect a polymer ring of any tested dose.' : ''}`);
   107	    return `<li>${esc(fixed)}</li>`;
   108	  }).join('');
   109	}

   111	export function pendingHtml(): string {
   112	  return '<p class="muted">The second-rung evidence has not been written yet (docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json). Run <code>node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --write</code> after the freeze.</p>';
   113	}

   115	/** First block that carries a free-energy table, from the fresh blocks if any ran, otherwise dev. */
   116	export function firstFreeBlock(ev: EvidenceFeView): { rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined; seed: number | undefined } {
   117	  const b = ev.fresh.candidates[0]?.blocks[0] ?? ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks[0];
   118	  return { rows: b?.freeBefore, seed: b?.seed };
   119	}

    88	      const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, s, fp);

    90	      const b = arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!;

    92	      const y = (offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(b, s, fp, 4, {}, offs);
    93	      const ya = y(A), yb = y(B);
    94	      f4 = Math.max(f4, Math.abs(ya.y - yb.y));
    95	      f1 = Math.max(f1, Math.abs(ya.ys[0] - yb.ys[0]));
    96	    }
    97	    // The R = 4 floor must be strictly below R = 1 scaled by R, and the control must not be degenerate (replay: f1 = 231, f4 = 500 < 924).

    99	    expect(f4).toBeLessThan(4 * f1);

   106	    const rule = (surv: number, R: number) => surv >= R - 1;

   154	    const pct = (qn: bigint, qd: bigint) => {

   156	      return Number((t.num * 10000n) / t.den) / 100;

   161	    expect(pct(19n, 20n)).toBeCloseTo(99.29, 1);
   162	    expect(binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).num * 65536n).toBe(697n * binomialUpperTail(16, 13, 1n, 2n).den);

     1	// I06b replicated Stage 2 (PLAN section 4.2): every arm's Stage 2 is run for R sim seeds (block seed + k * stride), the same
     2	// offsets in every arm, so injury steps and RNG keys coincide across arms. Stage 1 histories and transfers are shared.
     3	// Primary endpoint Y = integer sum over the R replicates of the receiver-labelled biomass at T2.
     4	import { cloneAudit, cloneState, NicheInvalid, snap, type Snap, type TransferRecord } from "./transfer.ts";
     5	import { buildArms, placeAll, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { runArm, historyCost, type ArmOutcome, type Hooks, type HistoryCost } from "./assay.ts";
     7	import { buildFreeMatched, freeRows, type FreeRow } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
     8	import { PROTOCOL_FE, candidateOf, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
     9	
    10	export const ARMS_FE = ["a", "b", "c", "cP", "cNP", "nb", "ab", "d0", "d1E", "d1B", "fU", "fS", "fB"] as const;
    11	export type ArmIdFe = (typeof ARMS_FE)[number] | string;
    12	
    13	export interface ArmOutcomeFe {
    14	  id: string;
    15	  /** Receiver-labelled B at T2 per replicate, and their sum Y. */
    16	  ys: number[];
    17	  y: number;
    18	  /** Replicates in which the receiver B at T2 was above zero. */
    19	  survivedReps: number;
    20	  /** Secondary, never gating: area under the receiver-B census curve summed over replicates (each curve: sum over the censuses after the start). */
    21	  auc: number;
    22	  builderB: number;
    23	  synthesis: number | null;
    24	  saturated: boolean;
    25	  growthAttributedReps: number;
    26	  energyEnd: string;
    27	  endHashes: string[];
    28	}
    29	
    30	export const seedFor = (seed: number, k: number, p: { replicateStride: number } = PROTOCOL_FE): number => seed + k * p.replicateStride;
    31	
    32	/** Run the same placed arm for R replicate sim seeds; every census of every replicate is audited to an exact zero residual (runArm throws otherwise). */
    33	export function runArmReplicated(x: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, R: number = p.R, hooks: Hooks = {}, offsets?: readonly number[]): ArmOutcomeFe {
    34	  const ks = offsets ?? Array.from({ length: R }, (_, k) => k);
    35	  const outs: ArmOutcome[] = ks.map((k) => {
    36	    const rep: PreArm = { id: x.id, state: cloneState(x.state, { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(seed, k, p) }), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit), records: [] };
    37	    return runArm(rep, p, hooks);
    38	  });
    39	  const sum = (f: (o: ArmOutcome) => number) => outs.reduce((t, o) => t + f(o), 0);
    40	  return {
    41	    id: x.id,
    42	    ys: outs.map((o) => o.y),
    43	    y: sum((o) => o.y),
    44	    survivedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.survived).length,
    45	    auc: sum((o) => o.receiverB.slice(1).reduce((t, v) => t + v, 0)),
    46	    builderB: sum((o) => o.builderB),
    47	    synthesis: x.id === "a" ? null : sum((o) => o.synthesis ?? 0),
    48	    saturated: outs.some((o) => o.saturated),
    49	    growthAttributedReps: outs.filter((o) => o.growthAttributed).length,
    50	    energyEnd: outs[0].energyEnd,
    51	    endHashes: outs.map((o) => o.endHash),
    52	  };
    53	}
    54	
    55	export interface ContrastsFe {
    56	  builder: number;
    57	  poly: number;
    58	  space: number;
    59	  /** Free-energy-matched: Y(b) - max over every constructed comparator (d1E, d1B, fU, fS, fB). */
    60	  inoc2: number;
    61	  /** First rung's contrast, reported next to inoc2 (summed-R units). */
    62	  inoc: number;
    63	  /** Reported, never gating: Y(b) minus the best of the three free-energy-matched comparators alone (fU, fS, fB), so the matching is read without the first rung's comparators in the max. */
    64	  inocMatched: number;
    65	  spaceP: number;
    66	  spaceNP: number;
    67	  naive: number;
    68	  /** Paired survival (replicates with receiver present): b minus the best comparator. Attribution only. */
    69	  survivalVsInoc2: number;
    70	}
    71	
    72	export function contrastsOfFe(o: Partial<Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>>): ContrastsFe {
    73	  const g = (id: string): ArmOutcomeFe => {
    74	    const v = o[id];
    75	    if (!v) throw new NicheInvalid(`arm ${id} missing`);
    76	    return v;
    77	  };
    78	  const yb = g("b").y;
    79	  const comps = ["d1E", "d1B", "fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => o[id]);
    80	  return {
    81	    builder: yb - g("nb").y,
    82	    poly: yb - g("ab").y,
    83	    space: yb - g("c").y,
    84	    inoc2: yb - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).y)),
    85	    inoc: yb - Math.max(g("d1E").y, g("d1B").y),
    86	    inocMatched: yb - Math.max(...["fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => o[id]).map((id) => g(id).y)),
    87	    spaceP: yb - g("cP").y,
    88	    spaceNP: yb - g("cNP").y,
    89	    naive: yb - g("d0").y,
    90	    survivalVsInoc2: g("b").survivedReps - Math.max(...comps.map((id) => g(id).survivedReps)),
    91	  };
    92	}
    93	
    94	export interface TransferLite {
    95	  op: string;
    96	  energyBefore: string;
    97	  energyAfter: string;
    98	  residual: string;
    99	  matterResidual: string;
   100	  detail: Record<string, number | string>;
   101	}
   102	export const liteTransfers = (rs: readonly TransferRecord[]): TransferLite[] => rs.map((r) => ({ op: r.op, energyBefore: r.before.energy, energyAfter: r.after.energy, residual: r.residual, matterResidual: r.matterResidual, detail: r.detail }));
   103	
   104	export interface BlockResultFe {
   105	  seed: number;
   106	  cluster: number;
   107	  valid: boolean;
   108	  invalidReason: string | null;
   109	  nPlaced: number;
   110	  dE: number;
   111	  dB: number;
   112	  dBFree: number;
   113	  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
   114	  costs: HistoryCost[];
   115	  /** Free energy and total energy per arm, before and after the shared inoculum placement. */
   116	  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
   117	  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
   118	  startTotals: Record<string, Snap>;
   119	  transfers: Record<string, TransferLite[]>;
   120	  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
   121	  contrasts: ContrastsFe | null;
   122	  /** Receiver survived (B > 0) in at least R - 1 of R replicates in the field arm b. */
   123	  viable: boolean;
   124	}
   125	
   126	/** One block: Stage 1 histories (shared), transfers, shared placement, free-energy-matched comparators, R replicates of every Stage 2 arm. An invalid block is reported, never dropped. */
   127	export function runBlockFe(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, hooks: Hooks = {}): BlockResultFe {
   128	  const base: BlockResultFe = { seed, cluster, valid: false, invalidReason: null, nPlaced: 0, dE: 0, dB: 0, dBFree: 0, refused: [], costs: [], freeBefore: [], freeAfter: [], startTotals: {}, transfers: {}, outcomes: {}, contrasts: null, viable: false };
   129	  let arms: PreArm[] = [];
   130	  try {
   131	    const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   132	    arms = built.arms;
   133	    base.dE = built.dE;
   134	    base.dB = built.dB;
   135	    base.costs = [historyCost("builder", built.histories.builder.state, p), historyCost("nonbuilder", built.histories.nonbuilder.state, p)];
   136	    const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!, p);
   137	    base.refused = fm.refused;
   138	    base.dBFree = fm.dB;
   139	    if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid(`every free-energy-matched comparator was refused: ${fm.refused.map((r) => `${r.id} (${r.reason})`).join("; ")}`);
   140	    arms = [...arms, ...fm.arms];
   141	    base.freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
   142	    base.nPlaced = placeAll(arms, p).length;
   143	    base.freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
   144	    for (const x of arms) {
   145	      base.startTotals[x.id] = snap(x.state, x.audit);
   146	      base.transfers[x.id] = liteTransfers(x.records);
   147	    }
   148	    for (const x of arms) base.outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R, hooks);
   149	    base.contrasts = contrastsOfFe(base.outcomes);
   150	    base.viable = base.outcomes.b.survivedReps >= p.R - 1;
   151	    base.valid = true;
   152	  } catch (e) {
   153	    if (!(e instanceof NicheInvalid)) throw e;
   154	    base.invalidReason = e.reason;
   155	    for (const x of arms) base.transfers[x.id] ??= liteTransfers(x.records);
   156	  }
   157	  return base;
   158	}
   159	
   160	/** Replicate-noise control for one block: the builder-removed (b) and nonbuilder-removed (nb) arms, Y over replicate set A = offsets 0..R-1 against a disjoint set B = offsets R..2R-1. */
   161	export function noiseBlock(cluster: number, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): { absBuilder: number; absNonbuilder: number } {
   162	  if (2 * p.R - 1 > p.maxOffsetIndex) throw new NicheInvalid(`R = ${p.R} needs offsets above the reserved ${p.maxOffsetIndex}; extend the registry reservation before escalating`);
   163	  const built = buildArms(candidateOf(cluster, p), seed, p);
   164	  placeAll(built.arms, p);
   165	  const A = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k), B = Array.from({ length: p.R }, (_, k) => k + p.R);
   166	  const y = (id: string, offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(built.arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!, seed, p, p.R, {}, offs).y;
   167	  return { absBuilder: Math.abs(y("b", A) - y("b", B)), absNonbuilder: Math.abs(y("nb", A) - y("nb", B)) };
   168	}
// I06b positive control at realistic scale (PLAN section 4.3): a hand-built polymer wall under the ORDINARY placement rule (no
// overwrite of polymer; same pool-cap and reserve guards), dose ladder ascending, the four gating contrasts per wall seed.
// W1 (ring around the footprint) is the only geometry that placement can legally accommodate; W2 (floor under the receiver) needs
// allowP and is probed here only to record why it is unavailable.
import { receiverAlone, placeAll } from "./arms.ts";
import { medianTimes2, signTestP } from "./decision.ts";
import { buildFreeMatched } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
import { buildWall, controlsAround, nominalBudget, wallCells } from "./fixtures.ts";
import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, type GatingFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
import { contrastsOfFe, runArmReplicated, type ArmOutcomeFe } from "./replicate.ts";
import { NicheInvalid, cloneAudit, cloneState } from "./transfer.ts";
import type { PreArm } from "./arms.ts";

export interface WallSeedResult {
  seed: number;
  contrasts: Record<GatingFe, number>;
  /** Y of the wall arm b (summed over replicates) and receiver-present replicates. */
  yWall: number;
  viableReps: number;
}

/** One wall seed at one dose: wall W1 through every arm and replicate under ordinary placement. */
export function wallGateBlockFe(seed: number, totalP: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): WallSeedResult {
  const pp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, wall: { ...p.wall, totalP } };
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, pp) }, seed, pp);
  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W1", pp), totalP, pp);
  const arms = [...controlsAround(wall, base, seed, pp)];
  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], pp);
  if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("every free-energy-matched comparator was refused");
  arms.push(...fm.arms);
  placeAll(arms, pp, { allowP: false });
  const out: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe> = {};
  for (const x of arms) out[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, pp, pp.R);
  const c = contrastsOfFe(out);
  return { seed, contrasts: { builder: c.builder, poly: c.poly, space: c.space, inoc2: c.inoc2 }, yWall: out.b.y, viableReps: out.b.survivedReps };
}

export interface DoseRow {
  totalP: number;
  seeds: readonly number[];
  results: WallSeedResult[];
  /** Per gating contrast: seeds positive and twice the median difference. */
  stats: Record<GatingFe, { k: number; p: { num: number; den: number }; medianTimes2: number }>;
  /** The gating contrast with the smallest median effect: where G-NOISE is read. */
  limiting: GatingFe;
  limitingMedianTimes2: number;
}

export function doseRow(totalP: number, results: WallSeedResult[]): DoseRow {
  const stats = Object.fromEntries(
    GATING_FE.map((k) => {
      const d = results.map((r) => r.contrasts[k]);
      const kk = d.filter((x) => x > 0).length;
      return [k, { k: kk, p: signTestP(d.length, kk), medianTimes2: medianTimes2(d) }];
    }),
  ) as DoseRow["stats"];
  const limiting = GATING_FE.reduce((m, k) => (stats[k].medianTimes2 < stats[m].medianTimes2 ? k : m), GATING_FE[0]);
  return { totalP, seeds: results.map((r) => r.seed), results, stats, limiting, limitingMedianTimes2: stats[limiting].medianTimes2 };
}

/** Wall pass at a dose for a candidate: every gating contrast positive in at least wallPassMin seeds and median >= delta. */
export function wallPasses(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
  return GATING_FE.every((k) => row.stats[k].k >= p.wallPassMin && row.stats[k].medianTimes2 >= 2 * delta);
}
/** G-NOISE: delta at most 1/noiseDivisor of the limiting contrast's median effect (twice-median integers: medianTimes2 >= 2 * noiseDivisor * delta). */
export function noiseGate(row: DoseRow, delta: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
  return row.limitingMedianTimes2 >= 2 * p.noiseDivisor * delta;
}

export interface Qualification {
  cluster: number;
  delta: number;
  /** 2 x the median dev builder polymer at T1 = twice-the-median integer builderPMedianTimes2. */
  ceiling: number;
  /** Smallest dose at which the wall passes ignoring the ceiling (the minimum detectable dose), or null if none on the ladder. */
  minDetectableDose: number | null;
  /** Smallest dose <= ceiling where the wall passes and G-NOISE holds, or null. */
  qualifyingDose: number | null;
  /** Doses <= ceiling on the ladder. */
  dosesWithinCeiling: number[];
  qualified: boolean;
  reason: string;
}

export function qualify(cluster: number, delta: number, builderPMedianTimes2: number, rows: readonly DoseRow[], p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): Qualification {
  const ceiling = builderPMedianTimes2;
  const within = rows.filter((r) => r.totalP <= ceiling);
  const mdd = rows.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
  const q = within.find((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p) && noiseGate(r, delta, p)) ?? null;
  const reason = q
    ? `W1 passes at ${q.totalP} (<= ceiling ${ceiling}) and delta ${delta} is at most half of the limiting contrast (${q.limiting}) median effect`
    : mdd === null
      ? `W1 is not detected at any ladder dose up to ${rows.length ? rows[rows.length - 1].totalP : 0} (passing needs ${p.wallPassMin} of ${p.wallSeeds.length} seeds positive on all four contrasts and median >= delta ${delta})`
      : mdd.totalP > ceiling
        ? `minimum detectable dose ${mdd.totalP} exceeds the realistic ceiling ${ceiling} (2 x dev builder polymer median)`
        : `W1 passes at ${mdd.totalP} but delta ${delta} exceeds half of the limiting contrast (${mdd.limiting}) median effect ${mdd.limitingMedianTimes2 / 2} (G-NOISE)`;
  return { cluster, delta, ceiling, minDetectableDose: mdd?.totalP ?? null, qualifyingDose: q?.totalP ?? null, dosesWithinCeiling: within.map((r) => r.totalP), qualified: q !== null, reason };
}

export interface LadderReport {
  geometry: "W1";
  rows: DoseRow[];
  w2: { status: "unavailable"; reason: string } | { status: "available"; reason: string };
}

/** Probe W2 (polymer floor under the receiver) under ordinary placement: expected to refuse, which is recorded as the reason it is unavailable. */
export function probeW2(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): LadderReport["w2"] {
  const seed = p.wallSeeds[0];
  const pp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, wall: { ...p.wall, totalP: p.doses[0] } };
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, pp) }, seed, pp);
  const wall: PreArm = { id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] };
  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W2", pp), p.doses[0], pp);
  try {
    placeAll([wall, base], pp, { allowP: false });
    return { status: "available", reason: "ordinary placement found enough common cells over the floor" };
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof NicheInvalid) return { status: "unavailable", reason: `ordinary placement refuses a polymer floor under the receiver: ${e.reason}` };
    throw e;
  }
}

/**
 * A candidate is settled once the ladder cannot teach more about it: it has qualified (a dose within its ceiling passes the wall
 * rule and G-NOISE), or it has a passing dose (its minimum detectable dose is known) and every dose within its ceiling has run.
 * Otherwise the ladder continues, to the top if need be, to state the minimum detectable dose or its absence.
 */
export function settled(rows: readonly DoseRow[], delta: number, ceiling: number, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): boolean {
  if (rows.some((r) => r.totalP <= ceiling && wallPasses(r, delta, p) && noiseGate(r, delta, p))) return true;
  const eligibleDone = p.doses.filter((d) => d <= ceiling).every((d) => rows.some((r) => r.totalP === d));
  return eligibleDone && rows.some((r) => wallPasses(r, delta, p));
}

/** Run the ladder ascending until every candidate is settled (see settled) or the ladder is exhausted. */
export function runLadder(deltas: Readonly<Record<string, number>>, ceilings: Readonly<Record<string, number>>, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, onDose?: (row: DoseRow) => void): LadderReport {
  const rows: DoseRow[] = [];
  for (const dose of p.doses) {
    const row = doseRow(dose, p.wallSeeds.map((s) => wallGateBlockFe(s, dose, p)));
    rows.push(row);
    onDose?.(row);
    if (Object.keys(deltas).every((c) => settled(rows, deltas[c], ceilings[c], p))) break;
  }
  return { geometry: "W1", rows, w2: probeW2(p) };
}
