# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

## What this is

An open-ended artificial-life ecology on WebGPU: closed in matter, open in energy, exact integer physics. Individuals, reproduction and heredity are *inferred* from local rules, never declared (no `Organism` class, no `reproduce()`). Research goal and milestones (M3 bootstrap → M4/M5 ensembles): `docs/plan.md`. Rules in prose plus the arithmetic-bounds argument: `docs/rules.md`.

The repo is a pnpm workspace (TypeScript packages + Vite lab), a Deno toolchain for headless GPU runs, and a Phoenix app (`apps/coordinator`). It is jj-colocated with git (`.jj/`).

## Commands

```bash
pnpm install
pnpm dev                 # lab at http://localhost:5173 (/selftest.html, /island.html)
bin/dev                  # lab + coordinator together (PORT, LAB_PORT override 4000/5173)
pnpm typecheck           # tsc over packages/*/src, apps/lab/src, tests (excludes tests/deno)
pnpm test                # vitest: apps/lab/test, packages/*/test, experiments/test, tools/test
pnpm vitest run packages/sim-ref/test/ref.test.ts -t "<name>"   # single test
deno run -A tests/deno/gpu_golden.ts    # GPU vs CPU reference, bit for bit (native WebGPU)
npx playwright test      # browser golden (tests/e2e), Chrome with WebGPU flags; BL_ALL_ENGINES=1 adds firefox/webkit
BL_E2E_PORT=5241 npx playwright test   # a second worktree on its own port (default 5174; strictPort, never reuses a server)
cd apps/coordinator && mix test         # or `mix test test/coordinator/queue_test.exs`
cd apps/coordinator && mix precommit    # warnings-as-errors, format, test
pnpm gen:prereg          # regenerate experiments/preregistration.md generated sections
pnpm gen:cards           # redraw the link-preview cards (site + one per world) with Chrome/WebGPU; then look at them
```

Run `pnpm install` before any `deno run` in a fresh checkout (deno 2.9 otherwise writes `workspaces` into package.json). Tests that start deno pass `--no-lock` (`tools/test/deno-no-lock.test.ts` checks; a launcher or coordinator a test starts that runs deno itself gets the PATH shim in `tests/deno/lib/no_lock_deno.ts`), but a top-level `deno run`, or a tool that starts deno, still writes a stale deno.lock: when package.json deps change, update it deliberately (`deno cache --frozen tools/stitch.ts` fails while it is stale).

`tests/deno/*.ts` are standalone scripts (not `Deno.test`); run each with `deno run -A tests/deno/<name>.ts`. Several need a real GPU; `coordinator_integration.ts` spawns `mix phx.server` against a scratch data dir.

Experiment CLIs (`tools/`, all Deno, native WebGPU): `run.ts` (write run bundles to `runs/<experiment>/<preset>/<condition>/seed-<n>/`), `analyze.ts` (ensemble analysis), `bootstrap.ts` (M3 MAP-Elites search), `retest.ts`, `calibrate.ts`, `island.ts` (headless island), `stitch.ts` (coordinator segments → bundles). Usage is in each file's header comment and in README.md. `runs/` is gitignored and can hold very large outputs.

## Architecture

**Package imports.** `@bl/*` resolve to `packages/*/src/index.ts` via four separate maps that must stay in sync: `tsconfig.json` paths, `vitest.config.ts` aliases, `deno.json` imports, and `apps/lab/vite.config.ts` aliases. There is no build step for packages.

**Two implementations of one rule set.**
- `packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts` is the executable specification (CPU).
- `packages/sim-gpu/src/shaders.ts` (WGSL) must match it bit for bit. `packages/sim-gpu/src/golden.ts` defines golden cases and runs on any WebGPU host (Deno, browser `/selftest.html`, Playwright).
- `packages/sim-ref/test/golden-hashes.test.ts` pins state hashes per `RULE_VERSION` (in `packages/schema/src/config.ts`). Any change to dynamics changes the hashes: an intended rule change bumps `RULE_VERSION` and re-pins deliberately. A change that isn't meant to alter dynamics must leave every pin untouched — new optional `WorldConfig` keys stay absent from `defaultConfig()` so existing configs hash identically.
- All arithmetic is integer (u32 matter, fixed-point controllers, counter-based PRNG keyed on seed/step/cell, stochastic rounding with remainders to the source). Conservation residual is exactly zero. Keep new arithmetic inside the bounds listed in `docs/rules.md` (`MATTER_MAX`, `POOL_MAX`, `mulShr` limits); `validateConfig`/`validateState` enforce them.

**Packages.** `schema` (config, presets, layout, genome, worlds, checkpoint encoding, migration/exchange, M3 founder set) → `sim-ref`, `sim-gpu` → `metrics` (census, tracker, collectives, activity, complexity, stats; runs on readbacks) → `runner` (conditions, `runExperiment`, observers, stitching, island client) and `search` (MAP-Elites, gates). `lineage` (schema, sim-ref) is the lineage inspector's genotype core: keys, ancestry, mutation replay, the controller probe, and the mutation-edge store the lab keeps. `runner` code runs unchanged in browsers and Deno.

**Checkpoints and replay.** A checkpoint artifact carries physics state and observer state together, under one digest. Segmented runs must equal a continuous run byte for byte (`tests/deno/segments.ts`, `tests/deno/stitch.ts`); this determinism is what makes distributed verification possible.

**Archipelago.** `apps/coordinator` (Phoenix, JSON API under `/api`, static status page `priv/static/index.html`) hands islands segment tasks. `Coordinator.Queue` does scheduling, `Coordinator.Segment` owns segment state transitions, and `Coordinator.Store` is content-addressed storage for checkpoints (`.blck`) and bundle files. A fraction of segments is re-run by a second island and compared by state hash. Islands are `apps/lab/island.html` (browser) or `tools/island.ts` (Deno), both via `packages/runner/src/island.ts`. `apps/coordinator/AGENTS.md` has Phoenix/Elixir/OTP conventions to follow there; everything between its `usage-rules` markers is generated by `mix usage_rules.sync` (config in `mix.exs`), so rerun that after dependency changes rather than editing the section. `mix usage_rules.search_docs "<term>" -p <pkg>` searches hexdocs for the coordinator's deps.

**Lab.** `apps/lab/src/sim.worker.ts` owns the WebGPU device, frame loop, rendering and OPFS storage. `apps/lab/src/execution.ts` owns census cadence, observer integrity, checkpoint settlement and replay traversal. State-changing messages run through `exclusive()`, which waits for in-flight frame execution; generation identity invalidates stale readbacks.

**Pre-registration.** `experiments/endpoints.ts` is the source of truth for primary endpoints, held-out observables and activity thresholds, and `tools/analyze.ts` executes it. The generated sections of `experiments/preregistration.md` (between `GENERATED` markers) come from `pnpm gen:prereg`, and `experiments/test/prereg-sync.test.ts` fails when they are stale. Edit `endpoints.ts` and regenerate; never hand-edit inside the markers. The doc is frozen (2026-09-27): its SHA-256 is recorded in `experiments/FROZEN`, `pnpm gen:prereg` now refuses to rewrite the generated sections, and every change goes in a dated amendment section.

**Analysis scale.** Run bundles can be hundreds of MB per run (for example `lineages.tsv`). Analysis tools stream per census rather than loading whole tables; keep new analysis code streaming.

## Codex reviews

Reviews by Codex use **Sol 6.1 at High effort**, not Astra (standing instruction from 2026-10-03, replaces the earlier `gpt-6-astra` policy). Run them read-only at phase boundaries and always before committing:

```bash
codex exec -m gpt-6.1-sol -c model_reasoning_effort=high -s read-only -C <checkout> - < prompt.md > review.txt
```

Use `< /dev/null` when the prompt is an argument (otherwise codex waits on stdin) and `-o <file>` for the final message. Historical "Astra review" comments in the code are provenance for past reviews; leave them. Delegated agents and workspaces follow the same rule.
import { configDefaults, defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const r = (p: string) => fileURLToPath(new URL(p, import.meta.url));

// The food-schedule-769 replays are kept out of git (docs/creative-prototypes/ARTIFACTS.md). Its test file is
// excluded rather than guarded while they are missing: the published replay pins this file's hash.
const schedule769Replays = existsSync(r("./docs/creative-prototypes/food-floor-longevity-replication-seed-1202.json"));

export default defineConfig({
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@bl/schema": r("./packages/schema/src/index.ts"),

      "@bl/metrics": r("./packages/metrics/src/index.ts"),
      "@bl/runner": r("./packages/runner/src/index.ts"),

    },

/**
 * I06b niche construction, second rung: free-energy-matched comparators, replicated readout, ordinary-placement dose-ladder positive control.
 * Invoked through the first rung's CLI so there is one tool: `node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe <mode>`:
 *   --bench          one block's step rate and the projected fresh run (informational)
 *   --calibrate      dev blocks, replicate-noise control and the wall dose ladder only: writes calibration.json
 *   --freeze         write protocol.lock.json (protocol hash, seeds, registry digest, calibration and source hashes) BEFORE any fresh run
 *   --write          fixtures and fresh blocks of qualified candidates (calibration is loaded frozen); needs a verifying lock and a pushed identical freeze
 *   --check          validate the saved artifacts without simulating
 *   --replay-check   regenerate the payload from the frozen calibration and require byte equality (same freeze guard)
 *   --repin-rung1    recompute only the source hashes in the first rung's manifest.json (nothing else of rung 1 changes)
 */
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync, readFileSync, renameSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { arch, platform } from "node:os";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import type * as NicheTool from "./niche.ts";

// The first rung's CLI dispatches here while its own top-level await is still pending, so this module must not import it statically
// (a cycle would never settle). mainFe receives the helpers; tests and other callers get them lazily.
type Helpers = Pick<typeof NicheTool, "sha256" | "registerAliases" | "sourceFiles">;
let helperModule: Helpers | undefined;
const helpers = async (): Promise<Helpers> => (helperModule ??= await import("./niche.ts"));
export const REPO = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../", import.meta.url));
const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => (await helpers()).sha256(text);

export const OUT_DIR_FE = `${REPO}docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/`;
export const EVIDENCE_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}evidence.json`;
export const MANIFEST_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}manifest.json`;
export const CALIBRATION_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}calibration.json`;
export const LOCK_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}protocol.lock.json`;
export const RUNG1_MANIFEST = `${REPO}docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01/manifest.json`;
export const BRANCH = "claude/i06-free-energy-matched";
export const OWNER = "i06b free-energy-matched";
const REGISTRY_REL = "experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json";
const rel = (abs: string) => abs.slice(REPO.length);

const writeAtomic = (path: string, text: string): void => {
  writeFileSync(`${path}.tmp`, text);
  renameSync(`${path}.tmp`, path);
};

const libFe = async () => {
  (await helpers()).registerAliases();
  const [evidence, protocol, schema, decision] = await Promise.all([
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/evidenceFe.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts"),
    import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/decisionFe.ts"),
  ]);
  const [replicate, dose] = await Promise.all([import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/replicate.ts"), import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/dose.ts")]);
  return { ...evidence, ...protocol, ...decision, ...replicate, qualify: dose.qualify, schema };
};

/** Digest over this rung's own reservation line(s) only: another slice's reservation never invalidates this evidence. */
export const registryDigestFe = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
  const text = readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8");
  const own = text.split("\n").filter((line) => line.includes(`"owner": "${OWNER}`));
  if (own.length !== 1) throw new Error(`expected exactly one registry reservation owned by "${OWNER}", found ${own.length}`);
  return sha256(own.join("\n"));
};

/** Every file whose bytes feed this rung's evidence: the first rung's source set plus this tool. */
export const frozenSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await helpers()).sourceFiles(root), "tools/niche/fe.ts"];
export const hashSources = async (root = REPO): Promise<Record<string, string>> => {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const f of await frozenSources(root)) out[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(root + f));
  return out;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Freeze-before-run guard

export type Git = (args: string[]) => string;
export const realGit: Git = (args) => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 26 });

/** Paths whose bytes the lock binds (or that the lock itself is): they must be committed and identical on the pushed branch. */
export const PLAN_FE = `${OUT_DIR_FE}PLAN.md`;
export const frozenPaths = async (root = REPO): Promise<string[]> => [...(await frozenSources(root)), REGISTRY_REL, rel(CALIBRATION_FE), rel(LOCK_FE), rel(PLAN_FE)];

/**
 * Refuse unless the freeze is clean and pushed: the frozen paths have no uncommitted change, and each path's blob on
 * origin/<BRANCH> equals the blob in the working tree (git ls-tree against git hash-object). The lock cannot name its own commit,
 * so this identical-on-the-pushed-branch check is the ordering proof. Returns problems (empty when the guard allows).
 */
export function freezeGuard(paths: string[], git: Git = realGit, branch = BRANCH): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  // The remote itself is asked (not a possibly stale remote-tracking ref); its commit must be present locally so its tree can be read.
  let ref = "";
  try {
    ref = git(["ls-remote", "--exit-code", "origin", `refs/heads/${branch}`]).split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "";
  } catch {
    return [`origin has no refs/heads/${branch} (or cannot be reached): push the freeze commit first (git push -u origin ${branch})`];
  }
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(ref)) return [`could not read the commit of origin refs/heads/${branch}`];
  let status = "";
  try {
    status = git(["status", "--porcelain", "--", ...paths]);
  } catch (e) {
    return [`git status failed: ${(e as Error).message}`];
  }
  for (const line of status.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) problems.push(`frozen file not committed clean: ${line.trim()}`);
  let tree = "";
  try {
    tree = git(["ls-tree", "-r", ref, "--", ...paths]);
  } catch {
    return [...problems, `commit ${ref} of origin/${branch} is not in the local object store: git fetch origin ${branch}`];
  }
  const remote = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const line of tree.split("\n").filter(Boolean)) {
    const m = /^\d+ blob ([0-9a-f]+)\t(.+)$/.exec(line);
    if (m) remote.set(m[2], m[1]);
  }
  const local = git(["hash-object", "--", ...paths]).split("\n").filter(Boolean);
  paths.forEach((path, i) => {
    if (!remote.has(path)) problems.push(`${path} is not on ${ref}`);
    else if (remote.get(path) !== local[i]) problems.push(`${path} differs from ${ref}`);
  });
  return problems;
}

/** Lock verification: protocol, calibration, seeds, registry digest and every source hash against the current tree. */
export async function verifyLock(planPath = PLAN_FE): Promise<string[]> {
  const L = await libFe();
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (!existsSync(LOCK_FE)) return ["no protocol.lock.json: run --calibrate and --freeze before the fresh run"];
  if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION_FE)) return ["no calibration.json"];
  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(LOCK_FE, "utf8"));
  const digest = await registryDigestFe();
  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
  if (lock.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("registry reservation changed since the freeze");
  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from protocol.lock.json");
  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE)))) problems.push("calibration.json changed since the freeze");
  if (lock.planSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(planPath)))) problems.push("PLAN.md (with Amendment 3) changed since the freeze");
  const now = await hashSources();
  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(lock.sources ?? {}), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (lock.sources?.[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} differs from the freeze`);
  return problems;
}

export async function guarded(git: Git = realGit): Promise<void> {
  const problems = [...(await verifyLock()), ...freezeGuard(await frozenPaths(), git)];
  if (problems.length) throw new Error(`refusing to read any fresh seed:\n${problems.join("\n")}`);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const logger = (t0: number) => (s: string) => console.log(`[${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s] ${s}`);

/** Every path that can simulate fresh seeds starts here, so the guard lives here: no lock, dirty tree or unpushed freeze means no simulation. */
export async function generateFe(git: Git = realGit): Promise<unknown> {
  await guarded(git);
  const L = await libFe();
  const dev = JSON.parse(readFileSync(CALIBRATION_FE, "utf8"));
  return L.buildPayloadFe(await registryDigestFe(), dev, L.PROTOCOL_FE, logger(Date.now()));
}

export async function makeManifestFe(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  return {
    kind: "niche-fe-evidence-manifest",
    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
    stepProjection: payload.stepProjection,
    sources: await hashSources(),
    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
  };
}

/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
export async function checkSavedFe(root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
  const L = await libFe();
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE_FE, manifest: MANIFEST_FE, calibration: CALIBRATION_FE, lock: LOCK_FE };
  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
  if (problems.length) return problems;
  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
  if (!L.verifySealFe(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
  const digest = await registryDigestFe(root);
  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("this rung's registry reservation changed since the evidence was written");
  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL_FE.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} is not the dev replicate-noise floor`);
  // Seed hygiene: every seed that can run lies in this rung's reservation, never in another owner's (rung 1's seeds included).
  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
  const all = L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE).map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
  for (const f of L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(OWNER)), OWNER)) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
  for (const s of all) if (!registry.some((r) => r.owner.startsWith(OWNER) && s.seed >= r.first && s.seed <= r.last)) problems.push(`seed ${s.seed} is outside this rung's reservation`);
  // Decision and qualification recomputation from the stored inputs.
  const recomputed = L.decideCandidatesFe(payload.fresh.inputs, L.PROTOCOL_FE.viableBlocksMin);
  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
  if (canon(L.panelVerdictFe(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
  const qual = payload.dev.candidates.map((c: any) => L.qualify(c.cluster, c.deltaProposed, c.builderPMedianTimes2, payload.dev.ladder.rows, L.PROTOCOL_FE));
  if (canon(qual) !== canon(payload.dev.qualification)) problems.push("qualification does not follow the stated positive-control rules");
  for (const q of payload.dev.qualification) {
    const sim = payload.fresh.candidates.some((c: any) => c.cluster === q.cluster);
    if (sim !== q.qualified) problems.push(`cluster ${q.cluster}: ${q.qualified ? "qualified but not simulated" : "not qualified but simulated"}`);
  }
  // Every ledger exactly zero.
  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
  // Sources.
  const now = await hashSources(root);
  if (canon(manifest.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(manifest.sources), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (manifest.sources[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} changed since the evidence was written`);
  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) problems.push("sources differ from the frozen lock");
  return problems;
}

export async function replayCheckFe(): Promise<string[]> {
  const fresh = serialize(await generateFe());
  return fresh === readFileSync(EVIDENCE_FE, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
}

    37	export function freeEnergyHtml(rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined, seed: number | undefined): string {
    38	  if (!rows?.length) return '<tr><td colspan="4">No block carries a free-energy table.</td></tr>';
    39	  const by = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
    40	  return FREE_ORDER.filter((id) => by.has(id)).map((id) => {
    41	    const r = by.get(id)!;
    42	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(COMPARATOR_TEXT[id] ?? id)}</th><td>${fmt(Number(r.freeE))}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.total))}</td><td>${r.id === 'b' ? 'reference' : `${esc(fmt(Number(r.constructionGap)))}: ${esc(GAP_WORD[r.flag] ?? r.flag)}`}</td></tr>`;

    58	export function doseLadderHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    59	  const n = ev.protocol.wallSeeds.length;
    60	  const rows = ev.dev.ladder.rows.map((r) => {
    61	    const gk = Object.entries(r.stats).map(([k, s]) => `${esc(k)} ${s.k}/${n}`).join(', ');
    62	    const passes = ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `c${q.cluster}: ${r.totalP <= q.ceiling ? 'within ceiling' : 'above ceiling'}`).join('; ');
    63	    return `<tr><th scope="row">W1 ring, polymer ${fmt(r.totalP)}</th><td>${gk}</td><td>${esc(r.limiting)} ${half(r.limitingMedianTimes2)}</td><td>${esc(passes)}</td></tr>`;
    64	  });
    65	  rows.push(`<tr><th scope="row">W2 floor under the receiver</th><td colspan="3">${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.status)}: ${esc(ev.dev.ladder.w2.reason)}</td></tr>`);
    66	  return rows.join('');
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function qualificationHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    70	  return ev.dev.qualification.map((q) => `<tr><th scope="row">cluster ${q.cluster}</th><td>${fmt(q.ceiling)}</td><td>${q.minDetectableDose === null ? 'none on the ladder' : fmt(q.minDetectableDose)}</td><td>${q.qualified ? `qualified at ${fmt(q.qualifyingDose)}` : 'not qualified'}</td><td>${esc(q.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('');
    71	}
    72	
    73	export function edgeFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    74	  return ev.fresh.results.map((r) => {
    75	    const e = edgeOf(r.label, r.mechanism);
    76	    const g = ['builder', 'poly', 'space', 'inoc2'].map((k) => (r.simulated ? `${esc(k)} ${r.gating[k].k}/${r.gating[k].n}${r.gating[k].passes ? '' : ' (no)'}` : `${esc(k)} not run`)).join(', ');
    77	    const word = r.simulated ? e.text : 'Not simulated: the positive control did not qualify this builder, so there is no negative and no positive here.';
    78	    return `<li class="edge-fe edge-${e.state}"><strong>${esc(r.id.replace('cluster-', 'Builder cluster '))}: edge ${esc(e.word)}.</strong> ${esc(word)}<br><span class="muted">Label <code>${esc(r.label)}</code>${r.reasons.length ? ` (${esc(r.reasons.join('; '))})` : ''}. Blocks positive: ${g}.</span></li>`;
    79	  }).join('');
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function farFieldHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    83	  const rows = ev.fixtures.reports.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${esc(f.naiveWouldSay)} (${f.naiveK} of ${f.seeds.length})</td><td>${f.kOld} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocFooledOld ? 'fooled' : 'not fooled'}</td><td>${f.kNew} of ${f.seeds.length}: ${f.inocCaughtNew ? 'caught' : 'not caught'}</td><td>${esc(f.fullGateLabel)}${f.caughtBy.length ? ` (rejected by ${esc(f.caughtBy.join(', '))})` : ''}</td></tr>`);
    84	  return rows.join('');
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function verdictFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
    88	  const f = ev.fresh;
    89	  const stopped = f.ran ? '' : '<p class="verdict-stop"><strong>Stopped before the fresh-seed run.</strong> No fresh block was simulated, so there are no per-candidate contrasts: this states how sensitive the assay is at realistic polymer doses, not that no niche is built.</p>';
    90	  return `<p class="verdict-text"><strong>${esc(f.panel.verdict.replaceAll('-', ' '))}.</strong> ${esc(f.panel.text)}</p>${stopped}
    91	    <ul>${f.caveats.map((c) => `<li>${esc(c)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    92	    <p class="muted">Protocol hash <code>${esc(ev.protocolHash.slice(0, 16))}</code>, seal <code>${esc(ev.integritySha256.slice(0, 16))}</code>. Each arm summed over ${ev.protocol.R} replicate sims; ${f.ran ? `${f.seeds.length} fresh blocks per qualified candidate` : 'no fresh blocks'}; ${ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks.length ?? 0} dev blocks per candidate were used for calibration only.</p>`;
    93	}
    94	
    95	const SHOWS_CLAIM = 'It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder\'s field can pass.';
    96	
    97	/**
    98	 * The limits list. The sealed text of the positive-control limit says the control "shows the assay can detect" a polymer structure; that holds
    99	 * only when a control passes. The sealed evidence is frozen, so the page restates that sentence conditionally and, when no candidate was
   100	 * qualified, says plainly that this run's control failed. (Correction recorded in docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/REVIEW.md.)
   101	 */
   102	export function limitsFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
   103	  const none = ev.dev.qualification.every((q) => !q.qualified);
   104	  return ev.limits.map((l) => {
   105	    if (!l.includes(SHOWS_CLAIM)) return `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`;
   106	    const fixed = l.replace(SHOWS_CLAIM, `A passing control would demonstrate that the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass.${none ? ' In this run no control passed at any dose: every qualification is false and no minimum detectable dose exists, so this run shows the assay could not detect a polymer ring of any tested dose.' : ''}`);
   107	    return `<li>${esc(fixed)}</li>`;
   108	  }).join('');
   109	}

   111	export function pendingHtml(): string {
   112	  return '<p class="muted">The second-rung evidence has not been written yet (docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/evidence.json). Run <code>node tools/niche/niche.ts --rung fe --write</code> after the freeze.</p>';
   113	}

   115	/** First block that carries a free-energy table, from the fresh blocks if any ran, otherwise dev. */
   116	export function firstFreeBlock(ev: EvidenceFeView): { rows: FreeRowView[] | undefined; seed: number | undefined } {
   117	  const b = ev.fresh.candidates[0]?.blocks[0] ?? ev.dev.candidates[0]?.blocks[0];
   118	  return { rows: b?.freeBefore, seed: b?.seed };
   119	}

describe("replicated readout", () => {
  it("replicate offsets are identical in every arm", () => {
    const b = placed();
    const seen: Record<string, Set<number>> = {};
    for (const id of ["b", "nb", "d0"]) {
      seen[id] = new Set();
      runArmReplicated(b.arms.find((x) => x.id === id)!, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2, { onStep: (s) => void seen[id].add(s.cfg.seed) });
    }
    const want = [seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 0), seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1)];
    for (const id of Object.keys(seen)) expect([...seen[id]].sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual(want);
  });

  it("Y is the integer sum of the R replicate endpoints; AUC is reported and never gating", () => {
    const b = placed();
    const o = runArmReplicated(b.arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2);
    expect(o.ys).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(o.y).toBe(o.ys[0] + o.ys[1]);
    expect(Number.isInteger(o.y) && Number.isInteger(o.auc)).toBe(true);
    expect(o.auc).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(GATING_FE as readonly string[]).not.toContain("auc");
  });

  it("replicate 0 is the rung-1 pipeline at the block seed", () => {
    const b = placed();
    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    const plain = runArm({ ...x, state: cloneState(x.state), audit: cloneAudit(x.audit) }, short);
    expect(runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 1).endHashes[0]).toBe(plain.endHash);
  });

  it("instrumented twin equals plain twin (stateHash) per replicate", () => {
    const b = placed();
    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    const plain = runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2);
    let calls = 0;
    const inst = runArmReplicated(x, short.devSeeds[1], short, 2, { onStep: () => void calls++ });
    expect(inst.endHashes).toEqual(plain.endHashes);
    expect(calls).toBe(2 * short.T2);
    const twin = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1) }));
    twin.run(short.T2);
    expect(plain.endHashes[1]).toBe(stateHash(twin.state));
  });

  it("deterministic replay: the same block twice gives equal bytes; every census of every replicate audits to zero", () => {
    const a = runBlockFe(0, short.devSeeds[1], short), b = runBlockFe(0, short.devSeeds[1], short);
    expect(a.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(canonicalJSON(a)).toBe(canonicalJSON(b));
    expect(Object.keys(a.outcomes).sort()).toEqual([...ARMS_FE].sort());
    for (const recs of Object.values(a.transfers)) for (const r of recs) expect([r.residual, r.matterResidual]).toEqual(["0", "0"]);
  }, 60_000);

  it("segmented stage 2 equals continuous at a replicate seed (checkpoint-free split of the same run)", () => {
    const b = placed();
    const x = b.arms.find((a) => a.id === "b")!;
    const cfg = { ...x.state.cfg, seed: seedFor(short.devSeeds[1], 1) };
    const cont = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, cfg));
    cont.run(short.T2);
    const half = new RefSim(cloneState(x.state, cfg));
    half.run(short.T2 / 2);
    half.run(short.T2 / 2);
    expect(stateHash(half.state)).toBe(stateHash(cont.state));
  });

  it("R=4 sum noise floor on a no-op control is below the R=1 floor scaled by R (dev-scale fixture with injury)", () => {
    const fp = { ...fixtureProtocolFe(), R: 4, injury: { period: 6, radius: 2, prob: 40_000_000, shell: 15 } };
    let f1 = 0, f4 = 0;
    for (const s of PROTOCOL_FE.devReplicateSeeds) {
      const { base, cond } = detritusField(s, fp);
      const arms = controlsAround(cond, base, s, fp);
      placeAll(arms, fp);
      const b = arms.find((x) => x.id === "b")!;
      const A = [0, 1, 2, 3], B = [4, 5, 6, 7];
      const y = (offs: number[]) => runArmReplicated(b, s, fp, 4, {}, offs);
      const ya = y(A), yb = y(B);
      f4 = Math.max(f4, Math.abs(ya.y - yb.y));
      f1 = Math.max(f1, Math.abs(ya.ys[0] - yb.ys[0]));
    }
    // The R = 4 floor must be strictly below R = 1 scaled by R, and the control must not be degenerate (replay: f1 = 231, f4 = 500 < 924).
    expect(f1).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(f4).toBeLessThan(4 * f1);
  }, 120_000);

  it("block viability is the R-1-of-R conversion of the field arm's surviving replicates (R = 4)", () => {
    const p4: ProtocolFe = { ...short, R: 4 };
    // The conversion lives inside runBlockFe (a frozen source), so it is checked there: viable must equal survivedReps >= R - 1 for each block,
    // and the survivedReps must be the count of positive replicate endpoints. Hand table for the rule itself:
    const rule = (surv: number, R: number) => surv >= R - 1;
    expect([2, 3, 4].map((k) => rule(k, 4))).toEqual([false, true, true]);
    for (const s of [PROTOCOL_FE.devSeeds[0], PROTOCOL_FE.devSeeds[1]]) {
      const r = runBlockFe(PROTOCOL_FE.panel[0], s, p4);
      if (!r.valid) continue;
      expect(r.outcomes.b.survivedReps).toBe(r.outcomes.b.ys.filter((y) => y > 0).length);
      expect(r.viable).toBe(rule(r.outcomes.b.survivedReps, 4));
    }
  }, 300_000);

  it("noiseBlock refuses an R that would leave the reserved replicate offsets", () => {
    expect(() => noiseBlock(0, PROTOCOL_FE.devReplicateSeeds[0], { ...PROTOCOL_FE, R: 8 })).toThrow(/reserved/);
  });

  it("runFeArms returns the second-rung contrasts with inoc2 at most inoc", () => {
    const fp = fixtureProtocolFe();
    const { base, cond } = detritusField(2, fp);
    const r = runFeArms(cond, base, 2, fp);
    expect(r.contrasts.inoc2).toBeLessThanOrEqual(r.contrasts.inoc);
    expect(r.outcomes.fU).toBeDefined();
  });
});

const inputOf = (id: string, k: number, n = 16, over: Partial<CandidateInputFe> = {}): CandidateInputFe => {
  const d = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => (i < k ? 100 : -100));
  return { id, status: "ok", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: d, poly: d, space: d, inoc2: d, spaceP: d, spaceNP: d, inoc: d, inocMatched: d, naive: d }, delta: 10, viableBlocks: 16, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [], ...over };
};

describe("second-rung decision", () => {

/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
export async function checkSavedFe(root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
  const L = await libFe();
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE_FE, manifest: MANIFEST_FE, calibration: CALIBRATION_FE, lock: LOCK_FE };
  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
  if (problems.length) return problems;
  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
  if (!L.verifySealFe(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
  const digest = await registryDigestFe(root);
  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("this rung's registry reservation changed since the evidence was written");
  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHashFe(digest, L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL_FE)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon(L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE))) problems.push("lock seed list differs from the protocol");
  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.dev)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the dev section of evidence.json");
  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
  for (const c of payload.dev.candidates) if (L.PROTOCOL_FE.delta[String(c.cluster)] !== c.deltaProposed) problems.push(`delta for cluster ${c.cluster} is not the dev replicate-noise floor`);
  // Seed hygiene: every seed that can run lies in this rung's reservation, never in another owner's (rung 1's seeds included).
  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}${REGISTRY_REL}`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
  const all = L.enumerateSeedsFe(L.PROTOCOL_FE).map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
  for (const f of L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(OWNER)), OWNER)) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
  for (const s of all) if (!registry.some((r) => r.owner.startsWith(OWNER) && s.seed >= r.first && s.seed <= r.last)) problems.push(`seed ${s.seed} is outside this rung's reservation`);
  // Decision and qualification recomputation from the stored inputs.
  const recomputed = L.decideCandidatesFe(payload.fresh.inputs, L.PROTOCOL_FE.viableBlocksMin);
  if (canon(recomputed) !== canon(payload.fresh.results)) problems.push("fresh results do not follow the stated decision rules");
  if (canon(L.panelVerdictFe(recomputed)) !== canon(payload.fresh.panel)) problems.push("panel verdict does not follow the stated rules");
  const qual = payload.dev.candidates.map((c: any) => L.qualify(c.cluster, c.deltaProposed, c.builderPMedianTimes2, payload.dev.ladder.rows, L.PROTOCOL_FE));
  if (canon(qual) !== canon(payload.dev.qualification)) problems.push("qualification does not follow the stated positive-control rules");
  for (const q of payload.dev.qualification) {
    const sim = payload.fresh.candidates.some((c: any) => c.cluster === q.cluster);
    if (sim !== q.qualified) problems.push(`cluster ${q.cluster}: ${q.qualified ? "qualified but not simulated" : "not qualified but simulated"}`);
  }
  // Every ledger exactly zero.
  const blocks = [...payload.dev.candidates, ...payload.fresh.candidates].flatMap((c: any) => c.blocks);
  for (const b of blocks) for (const [arm, recs] of Object.entries<any[]>(b.transfers)) for (const r of recs) if (r.residual !== "0" || r.matterResidual !== "0") problems.push(`block ${b.seed} arm ${arm} op ${r.op}: nonzero residual`);
  // Sources.
  const now = await hashSources(root);
  if (canon(manifest.sources) !== canon(now)) for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(manifest.sources), ...Object.keys(now)])) if (manifest.sources[f] !== now[f]) problems.push(`source ${f} changed since the evidence was written`);
  if (canon(lock.sources) !== canon(now)) problems.push("sources differ from the frozen lock");
  return problems;
}

export async function replayCheckFe(): Promise<string[]> {
  const fresh = serialize(await generateFe());
// I06b evidence assembly: dev (calibration) report, fresh (confirmation) report for qualified candidates only, fixtures, guards, the
// decision and the seal. Pure and deterministic; integers, integer rationals and decimal strings only. `--write` and `--replay-check`
// load the frozen calibration (dev) and regenerate only the fresh blocks and the fixtures.
import { canonicalJSON, sha256HexJS } from "@bl/schema";
import { medianTimes2 } from "./decision.ts";
import { decideCandidatesFe, panelVerdictFe, type CandidateInputFe, type CandidateResultFe, type PanelResultFe } from "./decisionFe.ts";
import { deltaOf } from "./evidence.ts";
import { runLadder, qualify, type LadderReport, type Qualification } from "./dose.ts";
import { fieldBlock, fixtureProtocolFe, decideFixtureFe, freeGapSeed, nullBlockFe, runGuardsFe, takeoverBlockFe, wallGateBlockFe2, type FieldVariant, type FixtureReportFe, type FreeGapSeed, type GuardFe } from "./fixturesFe.ts";
import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, enumerateSeedsFe, protocolHashFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
import { noiseBlock, runBlockFe, ARMS_FE, type BlockResultFe, type ContrastsFe } from "./replicate.ts";

export const EVIDENCE_VERSION_FE = "niche-fe-evidence-v1";

export interface DevCandidateFe {
  cluster: number;
  blocks: BlockResultFe[];
  noise: { absBuilder: number[]; absNonbuilder: number[] };
  deltaProposed: number;
  /** Twice the median over dev blocks of the builder history's total polymer at the end of conditioning (also the realistic ceiling 2 x median). */
  builderPMedianTimes2: number;
}

export interface DevReportFe {
  purpose: "calibration";
  R: number;
  seeds: readonly number[];
  candidates: DevCandidateFe[];
  ladder: LadderReport;
  qualification: Qualification[];
}

export function devReportFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): DevReportFe {
  const candidates: DevCandidateFe[] = p.panel.map((cluster) => {
    const blocks = p.devSeeds.map((s) => runBlockFe(cluster, s, p));
    const invalid = blocks.filter((b) => !b.valid);
    if (invalid.length) throw new Error(`cluster ${cluster}: invalid dev block(s) ${invalid.map((b) => `${b.seed} (${b.invalidReason})`).join(", ")}`);
    const noiseBlocks = p.devReplicateSeeds.map((s) => noiseBlock(cluster, s, p));
    const noise = { absBuilder: noiseBlocks.map((n) => n.absBuilder), absNonbuilder: noiseBlocks.map((n) => n.absNonbuilder) };
    const pMed2 = medianTimes2(blocks.map((b) => Number(b.costs.find((c) => c.kind === "builder")?.P ?? 0)));
    const deltaProposed = deltaOf(p.reserve.B * p.R, [...noise.absBuilder, ...noise.absNonbuilder]);
    log(`cluster ${cluster}: delta ${deltaProposed}, builder P median x2 ${pMed2}`);
    return { cluster, blocks, noise, deltaProposed, builderPMedianTimes2: pMed2 };
  });
  const deltas = Object.fromEntries(candidates.map((c) => [String(c.cluster), c.deltaProposed]));
  const ceilings = Object.fromEntries(candidates.map((c) => [String(c.cluster), c.builderPMedianTimes2]));
  const ladder = runLadder(deltas, ceilings, p, (row) => log(`dose ${row.totalP}: ${GATING_FE.map((k) => `${k} ${row.stats[k].k}/${p.wallSeeds.length} med ${row.stats[k].medianTimes2 / 2}`).join(", ")}`));
  const qualification = candidates.map((c) => qualify(c.cluster, c.deltaProposed, c.builderPMedianTimes2, ladder.rows, p));
  return { purpose: "calibration", R: p.R, seeds: p.devSeeds, candidates, ladder, qualification };
}

export interface FreshCandidateFe {
  cluster: number;
  blocks: BlockResultFe[];
  input: CandidateInputFe;
  /** Per arm: twice the median of Y (sum over replicates) over the fresh blocks. */
  medianYTimes2: Record<string, number | null>;
  survivedByArm: Record<string, { reps: number; of: number }>;
  /** Paired survival contrast: blocks where the field arm had strictly more surviving replicates than the best comparator (attribution only). */
  survivalPositiveBlocks: number;
  fluxAttribution: { available: boolean; reason: string; growthAttributedBlocks: number; coCulture: "unavailable" };
  /** Secondary, never gating: twice the median of the per-arm AUC. */
  aucMedianTimes2: Record<string, number | null>;
}

export interface FreshReportFe {
  purpose: "confirmation";
  seeds: readonly number[];
  /** false when no candidate qualified: no fresh seed was simulated. */
  ran: boolean;
  candidates: FreshCandidateFe[];
  inputs: CandidateInputFe[];
  results: CandidateResultFe[];
  panel: PanelResultFe;
  caveats: string[];
}

const med = (xs: readonly number[]): number | null => (xs.length ? medianTimes2(xs) : null);

export function freshReportFe(dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): FreshReportFe {
  const n = p.freshSeeds.length;
  const zeros = Array(n).fill(0);
  const candidates: FreshCandidateFe[] = [];
  const inputs: CandidateInputFe[] = [];
  for (const q of dev.qualification) {
    const delta = p.delta[String(q.cluster)];
    if (!q.qualified) {
      inputs.push({ id: `cluster-${q.cluster}`, status: "not-simulated", invalidReasons: [], contrasts: { builder: zeros, poly: zeros, space: zeros, inoc2: zeros, spaceP: zeros, spaceNP: zeros, inoc: zeros, inocMatched: zeros, naive: zeros }, delta, viableBlocks: 0, blocks: n, expectedBlocks: n, notSimulatedReasons: [q.reason] });
      continue;
    }
    const raw = p.freshSeeds.map((s) => runBlockFe(q.cluster, s, p));
    log(`cluster ${q.cluster}: fresh ${raw.filter((b) => b.valid).length}/${n} valid`);
    const col = (k: keyof ContrastsFe) => raw.map((b) => (b.contrasts ? b.contrasts[k] : 0));
    const valid = raw.filter((b) => b.valid).length;
    const input: CandidateInputFe = {
      id: `cluster-${q.cluster}`,
      status: valid === n ? "ok" : "invalid",
      invalidReasons: raw.filter((b) => !b.valid).map((b) => `seed ${b.seed}: ${b.invalidReason}`),
      contrasts: { builder: col("builder"), poly: col("poly"), space: col("space"), inoc2: col("inoc2"), spaceP: col("spaceP"), spaceNP: col("spaceNP"), inoc: col("inoc"), inocMatched: col("inocMatched"), naive: col("naive") },
      delta,
      viableBlocks: raw.filter((b) => b.viable).length,
      blocks: valid,
      expectedBlocks: n,
      notSimulatedReasons: [],
    };
    inputs.push(input);
    const sat = raw.filter((b) => Object.values(b.outcomes).some((o) => o.saturated)).length;
    candidates.push({
      cluster: q.cluster,
      blocks: raw,
      input,
      medianYTimes2: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, med(raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).map((b) => b.outcomes[a].y))])),
      survivedByArm: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, { reps: raw.reduce((t, b) => t + (b.outcomes[a]?.survivedReps ?? 0), 0), of: raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).length * p.R }])),
      survivalPositiveBlocks: raw.filter((b) => (b.contrasts?.survivalVsInoc2 ?? 0) > 0).length,
      fluxAttribution: valid === 0 ? { available: false, reason: "no valid block produced a measurement", growthAttributedBlocks: 0, coCulture: "unavailable" } : sat === 0 ? { available: true, reason: "no role counter reached saturation in any replicate; the co-culture arm (a) is never attributed", growthAttributedBlocks: raw.filter((b) => (b.outcomes.b?.growthAttributedReps ?? 0) * 2 >= p.R).length, coCulture: "unavailable" } : { available: false, reason: `a role counter reached ${p.roleSaturation} in ${sat} block(s)`, growthAttributedBlocks: 0, coCulture: "unavailable" },
      aucMedianTimes2: Object.fromEntries(ARMS_FE.map((a) => [a, med(raw.filter((b) => b.outcomes[a]).map((b) => b.outcomes[a].auc))])),
    });
  }
  const results = decideCandidatesFe(inputs, p.viableBlocksMin);
  const caveats = [p.powerStatement];
  if (candidates.length === 0) caveats.push("No candidate qualified on the dev positive control, so no fresh seed was simulated: this is a statement about the sensitivity of the assay at realistic polymer doses, not about niche construction.");
  return { purpose: "confirmation", seeds: p.freshSeeds, ran: candidates.length > 0, candidates, inputs, results, panel: panelVerdictFe(results), caveats };
}

export interface FixturesReportFe {
  protocol: { grid: number; kernelRadius: number; T2: number; R: number; reserve: { B: number; E: number }; delta: number };
  seeds: readonly number[];
  reports: FixtureReportFe[];
  freeGap: FreeGapSeed[];
  guards: GuardFe[];
  /** Rung-1 W2 wall (allowP) at the frozen scale through the second-rung arms; diagnostic, never qualifying. */
  handWallGate: FixtureReportFe;
}

export function fixturesReportFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): FixturesReportFe {
  const fp = fixtureProtocolFe(p);
  const seeds = p.fixtureSeeds;
  const delta = Math.ceil((fp.reserve.B * fp.R) / 50);
  const reports: FixtureReportFe[] = [];
  for (const v of ["far-field", "uniform", "footprint"] as FieldVariant[]) reports.push(decideFixtureFe(v === "far-field" ? "far-field-energy" : `energy-${v}`, seeds, seeds.map((s) => fieldBlock(v, s, fp)), delta));
  reports.push(decideFixtureFe("takeover-only", seeds, seeds.map((s) => takeoverBlockFe(s, fp)), delta));
  reports.push(decideFixtureFe("null-field", seeds, seeds.map((s) => nullBlockFe(s, fp)), delta));
  const wp: ProtocolFe = { ...p, R: p.fixtureR };
  const handWallGate = decideFixtureFe("hand-wall-gate-W2", seeds, seeds.map((s) => wallGateBlockFe2(s, wp)), Math.ceil((p.reserve.B * p.fixtureR) / 50));
  return { protocol: { grid: fp.grid, kernelRadius: fp.kernelRadius, T2: fp.T2, R: fp.R, reserve: fp.reserve, delta }, seeds, reports, freeGap: seeds.map((s) => freeGapSeed(s, fp)), guards: runGuardsFe(fp), handWallGate };
}

export function stepProjectionFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): { formula: string; freshBlocks: number; stepsPerBlock: number; devBlocks: number } {
  const perBlock = 2 * p.T1 + 13 * p.R * p.T2;
  return { formula: "per block 2*T1 + 13 arms * R * T2; dev blocks, replicate-noise, wall ladder (12 arms * R * T2 per wall seed per dose) and fixtures are calibration or unit scale", freshBlocks: p.panel.length * p.freshSeeds.length, stepsPerBlock: perBlock, devBlocks: p.panel.length * p.devSeeds.length };
}

export interface PayloadFe {
  kind: "niche-fe-evidence";
  version: string;
  protocol: ProtocolFe;
  protocolHash: string;
  registryDigest: string;
  seedCount: number;
  stepProjection: ReturnType<typeof stepProjectionFe>;
  dev: DevReportFe;
  fresh: FreshReportFe;
  fixtures: FixturesReportFe;
  powerStatement: string;
  limits: string[];
  amendments: string[];
  integritySha256: string;
}

export const LIMITS_FE: readonly string[] = [
  "Exploratory, CPU only (sim-ref), mutation off. No GPU evidence, no physics change, not part of the frozen preregistration.",
  "Free energy cannot be matched together with total energy at equal matter: fU, fS and fB match free E exactly and carry a signed, evidence-only constructionGap in total energy. They sit on the weaker side of total energy, so they screen free-energy alone, not bound inventories.",
  "The positive control is a hand-built polymer ring under ordinary placement (W1). It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass. The polymer floor under the receiver (W2) is outside ordinary placement and is reported unavailable.",
  "Sixteen fresh blocks with exact sign tests can only certify a benefit that is positive in about nine of ten blocks or more and clears delta on four contrasts and viability. A negative is a failure to detect such a benefit under these rules, not an exclusion bound on the effect size and not evidence that there is no benefit.",
  "Summing R replicates reduces assay-seed noise, not block-to-block field variance; the block count carries that.",
  "A negative does not say a second link is impossible, does not test mutation reaching builder or receiver, and does not test A+B->C. One receiver genome, one field per candidate, T1 = 60, T2 = 80, 24 x 24, single tile.",
  "Receiver-labelled biomass inventory can rise by relabelling detritus; growth is claimed only where flux attribution is available and synthesis is at least half the gain. Survival is a viability requirement on the field arm, not a claimed benefit.",
  "The ablation arm is analytical (polymer converted to waste at transfer, released energy booked as named heat); co-culture (arm a) flux attribution is unavailable.",
  "Builder founder-biomass dose response and a polymer-bearing full-gate trap fixture are left to a later rung.",
];

export function amendmentsFe(dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe): string[] {
  const d = dev.candidates.map((c) => `cluster ${c.cluster}: delta ${p.delta[String(c.cluster)]}, ceiling ${c.builderPMedianTimes2}`).join("; ");
  return [`Amendment 3 (2026-11-02, PLAN section 10): R = ${p.R}; doses ${p.doses.join(", ")}; ${d}; block, replicate, noise, wall and fixture seeds as enumerated in the protocol (${enumerateSeedsFe(p).length} seeds); frozen and pushed before any fresh seed was read. Rung 1 (docs/niche-construction-2026-11-01) is superseded only by a rung-2 label, not rewritten.`];
}

export function sealPayloadFe(body: Omit<PayloadFe, "integritySha256">): PayloadFe {
  return { ...body, integritySha256: sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body))) };
}
export function verifySealFe(p: PayloadFe): boolean {
  const { integritySha256, ...body } = p;
  return integritySha256 === sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(body)));
}

/** Build the sealed payload around a FROZEN dev report (calibration.json): only the fixtures and the fresh blocks are simulated here. */
export function buildPayloadFe(registryDigest: string, dev: DevReportFe, p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE, log: (s: string) => void = () => {}): PayloadFe {
  const fresh = freshReportFe(dev, p, log);
  return sealPayloadFe({ kind: "niche-fe-evidence", version: EVIDENCE_VERSION_FE, protocol: p, protocolHash: protocolHashFe(registryDigest, p), registryDigest, seedCount: enumerateSeedsFe(p).length, stepProjection: stepProjectionFe(p), dev, fresh, fixtures: fixturesReportFe(p), powerStatement: p.powerStatement, limits: [...LIMITS_FE], amendments: amendmentsFe(dev, p) });
}

// I06b fixtures (PLAN section 4.5), 16x16 at unit scale. far-field-energy: a conditioned field that differs from its controls only by
// extra free energy laid in a band OUTSIDE the inoculum footprint plus halo (no polymer). The first rung's inoculum contrast
// (D_inoc: d1E, d1B only) can be fooled by it because those comparators read energy inside the region only; D_inoc2 adds fU/fS/fB.
// Ablation only converts P to C, so with no polymer D_poly = 0 and the full gate rejects it: what is tested is the inoculum contrast.
import { CH, cellCount, type Genome, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
import { disc, footprintCells, outsideRegion, placeAll, receiverAlone, regionCells, type PreArm } from "./arms.ts";
import { ALPHA, contrastResult, medianTimes2 } from "./decision.ts";
import { decideCandidatesFe, type CandidateResultFe } from "./decisionFe.ts";
import { buildFreeMatched, freeRows, fundBiomass, type FreeRow } from "./freeEnergy.ts";
import { buildWall, controlsAround, nominalBudget, regionCellsExcludingFootprint, uniformBudget, wallCells, inertReceiver } from "./fixtures.ts";
import { GATING_FE, PROTOCOL_FE, type GatingFe, type ProtocolFe } from "./protocol.ts";
import { contrastsOfFe, runArmReplicated, type ArmOutcomeFe, type ContrastsFe } from "./replicate.ts";
import { NicheInvalid, audited, cloneAudit, cloneState, debitEven } from "./transfer.ts";

export function fixtureProtocolFe(p: ProtocolFe = PROTOCOL_FE): ProtocolFe {
  return { ...p, grid: 16, T1: 0, T2: 40, injury: null, footprint: { x: 8, y: 8, radius: 2 }, halo: 2, nMin: 8, reserve: { B: 480, E: 24 }, nutrient: 100, R: p.fixtureR };
}

const setChannel = (s: WorldState, ch: number, cells: readonly number[], total: number): void => {
  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), m = cells.length, base = Math.floor(total / m), rem = total % m;
  cells.forEach((i, j) => (s.cells[ch * n + i] += base + (j < rem ? 1 : 0)));
};

export interface FeBlock {
  outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe>;
  contrasts: ContrastsFe;
  freeBefore: FreeRow[];
  freeAfter: FreeRow[];
  refused: { id: string; reason: string }[];
}

/** Controls around a hand-built conditioned arm `b`, plus fU/fS/fB; shared placement; R replicates of every arm. */
export function runFeArms(b: PreArm, d0: PreArm, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, opts: { allowP?: boolean; genome?: Genome } = {}): FeBlock {
  const arms = [...controlsAround(b, d0, seed, p)];
  const fm = buildFreeMatched(arms[0], p);
  if (fm.arms.length === 0) throw new NicheInvalid("every free-energy-matched comparator was refused");
  arms.push(...fm.arms);
  const freeBefore = freeRows(arms);
  placeAll(arms, p, opts);
  const freeAfter = freeRows(arms);
  const outcomes: Record<string, ArmOutcomeFe> = {};
  for (const x of arms) outcomes[x.id] = runArmReplicated(x, seed, p, p.R);
  return { outcomes, contrasts: contrastsOfFe(outcomes), freeBefore, freeAfter, refused: fm.refused };
}

const condOf = (base: PreArm): PreArm => ({ id: "b", state: cloneState(base.state), audit: cloneAudit(base.audit), records: [] });

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// energy fields

export type FieldVariant = "far-field" | "uniform" | "footprint";
/** Extra stored free energy, sized so the naive metric clears the fixture delta. */
export const FIELD_GIFT: Record<FieldVariant, number> = { "far-field": 10000, uniform: 10000, footprint: 4000 };

/** Cells of the far-field band: outside the footprint-plus-halo region, within two more cells of radius. */
export function bandCells(p: ProtocolFe): number[] {
  const inR = new Set(regionCells(p));
  return disc(p.footprint.x, p.footprint.y, p.footprint.radius + p.halo + 2, p.grid, p.grid).filter((i) => !inR.has(i));
}

export function fieldBlock(variant: FieldVariant, seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, gift = FIELD_GIFT[variant]): FeBlock {
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
  const cond = condOf(base);
  const cells = variant === "far-field" ? bandCells(p) : variant === "uniform" ? Array.from({ length: p.grid * p.grid }, (_, i) => i) : footprintCells(p);
  setChannel(cond.state, CH.E, cells, gift);
  cond.audit.energyStart += BigInt(gift);
  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p);
}

/** Non-living detritus B on the halo ring (funded from A there and from E outside): bound energy instead of free, equal totals with receiver alone. An inert receiver is placed. */
export function detritusField(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe, detritus = 200): { base: PreArm; cond: PreArm } {
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
  const cond = condOf(base);
  const ring = regionCellsExcludingFootprint(p);
  audited("detritus", cond.state, cond.audit, () => {
    const s = cond.state;
    debitEven(s, CH.A, detritus, ring);
    setChannel(s, CH.B, ring, detritus);
    debitEven(s, CH.E, (s.cfg.eB - s.cfg.eA) * detritus, outsideRegion(p));
    return { detritus };
  });
  return { base, cond };
}
export const takeoverBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
  const { base, cond } = detritusField(seed, p);
  return runFeArms(cond, base, seed, p, { genome: inertReceiver(p) });
};
export const nullBlockFe = (seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock => {
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: uniformBudget(p) }, seed, p);
  return runFeArms(condOf(base), base, seed, p);
};

/** The rung-1 hand wall W2 (polymer floor under the receiver, allowP) at the frozen scale through the second-rung arms: a diagnostic, never qualifying. */
export function wallGateBlockFe2(seed: number, p: ProtocolFe): FeBlock {
  const base = receiverAlone({ audit: nominalBudget(seed, p) }, seed, p);
  const wall = condOf(base);
  buildWall(wall, wallCells("W2", p), p.wall.totalP, p);
  return runFeArms(wall, base, seed, p, { allowP: true });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// free-gap-replay

export interface FreeGapSeed {
  seed: number;
  /** Rung-1 observed direction: receiver alone holds MORE free E than b with equal totals. */
  d0FreeE: string;
  bFreeE: string;
  d0Total: string;
  bTotal: string;
  d0HoldsMoreFree: boolean;
  totalsEqual: boolean;
  /** Free-energy difference to b for each comparator, before and after placement (all zero). */
  freeDiffBefore: Record<string, string>;
  freeDiffAfter: Record<string, string>;
  constructionGap: Record<string, string>;
  flags: Record<string, string>;
  residualsZero: boolean;
}

  const ids = ["fU", "fS", "fB"].filter((id) => b.before.some((r) => r.id === id));

  --experimental.importDurations.failOnDanger                Fail the test run if any import exceeds the danger threshold (default: false).

/**
 * I06 niche construction evidence: the builder->receiver dependency edge, first causal rung. CPU only (sim-ref), Node 24 (relative
 * imports only; @bl/* is resolved by the hook below, as in tools/predictability/atlas.ts):
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --bench          step rate on this machine and the projected regeneration time (informational)
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --calibrate      dev seeds only: writes calibration.json (delta floors, polymer adequacy, hand-wall G3)
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --freeze         write protocol.lock.json (protocol hash, seed lists, registry digest) BEFORE any fresh run
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --write          write evidence.json and manifest.json (once, deliberately; needs a matching lock)
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --check          validate the saved artifacts without simulating
 *   node tools/niche/niche.ts --replay-check   regenerate the payload and require byte equality
 */

const writeAtomic = (path: string, text: string): void => {
  writeFileSync(`${path}.tmp`, text);
  renameSync(`${path}.tmp`, path);
};

/** PLAN G6: regeneration (blocks, dev wall controls and fixtures) should take under five minutes on an unloaded machine. Load-dependent, so over-target is reported loudly rather than failing a replay. */
export const RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS = 300;
const runtimeNote = (seconds: number): string => (seconds > RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS ? ` WARNING: over the ${RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS} s target (G6); check machine load before trusting the budget` : ` (within the ${RUNTIME_TARGET_SECONDS} s target)`);
const SRC_DIR = "packages/runner/src/niche/";

let hooked = false;
export function registerAliases(): void {
  if (hooked || process.env.VITEST) return;
  hooked = true;
  try {
    registerHooks({
      resolve(specifier, context, next) {
        const m = /^@bl\/(.+)$/.exec(specifier);
        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
      },
    });
  } catch { /* already running under a loader that resolves @bl/* (vitest) */ }
}

const lib = async () => {
  registerAliases();
  const [evidence, protocol, schema, decision, assay, fixtures] = await Promise.all([
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/evidence.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/protocol.ts"),
    import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/decision.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/assay.ts"),
    import("../../packages/runner/src/niche/fixtures.ts"),
  ]);
  return { ...evidence, ...protocol, ...decision, ...assay, FIXTURE_SEEDS: fixtures.FIXTURE_SEEDS, schema };
};

export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
export const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => {
  const { schema } = await lib();
  return schema.sha256HexJS(typeof text === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(text) : text);
};
/**
 * Reservations made by slices after this one are not part of this protocol's registry binding: the digest is of the registry as it stood
 * when this slice froze, so a later slice reserving its own seeds does not invalidate this evidence. Collisions with those reservations
 * are still checked against the live registry below. A further slice adds its owner prefix here (or scopes its own digest to its own reservations, as I08 does).
 */
const LATER_SLICE_OWNERS = ["i08 moving light", "i06b free-energy-matched"];
export const registryDigest = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => {
  const text = readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8");
  return sha256(text.split("\n").filter((line) => !LATER_SLICE_OWNERS.some((o) => line.includes(`"owner": "${o}`))).join("\n"));
};

    95	const SHOWS_CLAIM = 'It shows the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder\'s field can pass.';

    97	/**
    98	 * The limits list. The sealed text of the positive-control limit says the control "shows the assay can detect" a polymer structure; that holds
    99	 * only when a control passes. The sealed evidence is frozen, so the page restates that sentence conditionally and, when no candidate was
   100	 * qualified, says plainly that this run's control failed. (Correction recorded in docs/niche-construction-fe-2026-11-02/REVIEW.md.)
   101	 */
   102	export function limitsFeHtml(ev: EvidenceFeView): string {
   103	  const none = ev.dev.qualification.every((q) => !q.qualified);
   104	  return ev.limits.map((l) => {
   105	    if (!l.includes(SHOWS_CLAIM)) return `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`;
   106	    const fixed = l.replace(SHOWS_CLAIM, `A passing control would demonstrate that the assay can detect a polymer structure of that dose, not that a builder's field can pass.${none ? ' In this run no control passed at any dose: every qualification is false and no minimum detectable dose exists, so this run shows the assay could not detect a polymer ring of any tested dose.' : ''}`);
   107	    return `<li>${esc(fixed)}</li>`;
   108	  }).join('');
